By leveraging the strongly-convex-like property of the quadratic penalty function and the proximal smoothness of the Stiefel manifold, we establish global convergence guarantees with the best-known iteration complexities under both constant and diminishing step sizes. Then, we reformulate the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning problem for large language models as a manifold optimization problem, introducing Manifold-LoRA for geometry-accelerated adaptation. This approach employs the proposed landing technique and a carefully designed step size strategy to accelerate the training process. Numerical experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the efficiency and strong downstream performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [164] [Guiding Posterior Exploration with Optimizer-Derived Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25312)
*Moritz Schlager,Emanuel Sommer,Thomas Möllenhoff,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于优化器曲率估计的预条件采样策略，利用AdamW等自适应优化器在热身阶段计算的曲率信息来指导贝叶斯深度集成的采样，从而减少或消除采样燃烧期，提高数值稳定性，且不增加额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络中采样方法面临高维和多模态后验分布的计算成本高的问题，而贝叶斯深度集成（从多个优化解热启动采样）是一种有效策略，但仍需较长的燃烧期。本文旨在利用热身阶段优化器计算的曲率信息来改进采样效率。

Method: 提出预条件采样策略，利用AdamW等自适应优化器在热身阶段计算的曲率估计（作为副产品）来构造预条件矩阵，从而指导采样相位，减少或消除燃烧期，提升数值稳定性。

Result: 该方法在不增加额外计算成本的情况下，显著减少或消除了采样燃烧期，提高了数值稳定性，并保持或提升了预测性能和不确定性量化效果。在多个数据集和网络架构上验证了一致性。

Conclusion: 基于优化器曲率的预条件采样策略是一种高效且实用的贝叶斯深度集成方法，能够在不增加计算负担的前提下改善采样效率与稳定性，并保持良好性能。

Abstract: Sampling-based methods offer a principled approach to uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks. Their practical use, however, is often challenged by the computational cost of exploring high-dimensional and multimodal posterior distributions. To overcome these difficulties, Bayesian Deep Ensembles, i.e., warmstarting the sampling from several optimized solutions, have proven to be an effective strategy. In this paper, we demonstrate that curvature estimates computed during the warmstart as a byproduct in adaptive optimizers such as AdamW can inform the sampling phase at negligible additional cost. Specifically, our proposed preconditioned sampling strategy based on optimizer-derived geometries can substantially reduce or even eliminate the need for a lengthy sampling burn-in phase and leads to greater numerical stability. This approach consistently maintains or improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification without any additional computational costs. We confirm the consistency of our findings across various datasets and network architectures.

</details>


### [165] [Explainable AI for Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction Using Simulated Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25348)
*Md Zahid Hasan Ontor,Md Al Amin,Anik Dev Nath,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习（FL）和投票分类器（VotingClassifier）的慢性肾脏病（CKD）早期预测方法，通过随机森林、AdaBoost和XGBoost三种模型在客户端进行训练，并使用GridSearchCV优化模型性能，在全局服务器上通过投票集成获得最佳模型。同时引入可解释人工智能（XAI）技术提高模型透明度。全局模型平均准确率达到99%，展示了可解释联邦学习模型在早期CKD诊断中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾脏病（CKD）是重要的公共卫生挑战，早期检测对于预防严重并发症和改善患者预后至关重要。现有的预测模型可能面临数据隐私问题，且缺乏可解释性。因此，本研究旨在利用联邦学习在保护数据隐私的同时，结合多种机器学习模型和可解释AI技术，实现高精度且透明的CKD早期诊断。

Method: 使用联邦学习框架，每个客户端本地训练随机森林、AdaBoost和XGBoost三种模型，并通过GridSearchCV进行超参数优化。客户端将模型参数上传至全局服务器，服务器采用投票分类器（VotingClassifier）集成各客户端模型，选择最佳拟合模型作为全局模型。此外，引入可解释人工智能（XAI）技术对预测机制进行解释，增强模型透明度和可信度。

Result: 全局模型的平均准确率达到99%，表明联邦学习结合投票集成策略能够有效预测CKD，且模型性能优异。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了可解释联邦学习模型在支持早期CKD诊断方面的潜力，为数据驱动的医疗解决方案提供了新思路，有助于在保护数据隐私的同时实现高精度、可解释的疾病预测。

Abstract: Chronic Kidney Disease (CKD), characterized by the gradual loss of kidney function, remains a significant public health challenge. Early detection is crucial for preventing severe complications and enhancing patient outcomes. In this study, Federated Learning (FL) with a VotingClassifier was used to predict CKD using a clinical dataset, where Random Forest, AdaBoost, and XGBoost were utilized to compare and identify the best-fitting model for the global server. Additionally, GridSearchCV was applied to optimize the models' performance on the client's side. To enhance model transparency and trustworthiness, explainable AI (XAI) techniques were incorporated to interpret the prediction mechanisms. The global model's average accuracy was 99%, highlighting the potential of interpretable FL models in supporting early CKD diagnosis and advancing data-driven healthcare solutions.

</details>


### [166] [Raven: High-Recall Sequence Modeling with Sparse Memory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25357)
*Arshia Afzal,Aviv Bick,Eric P. Xing,Volkan Cevher,Albert Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Raven，一种线性时间序列模型，通过固定数量的记忆槽和基于输入的路由机制，在密集更新（如状态空间模型）和稀疏写入（如滑动窗口注意力）之间取得平衡，从而在长上下文召回任务中显著优于现有线性时间基线模型，并能外推到训练长度16倍的上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性时间序列模型在处理长上下文召回时面临权衡：基于状态的模型（如SSM和线性Transformer）密集更新整个状态，导致干扰；滑动窗口注意力（SWA）稀疏写入但仅保留固定窗口，一旦相关标记被驱逐则召回下降。现有方法难以同时兼顾长距离内容保持和高效计算。

Method: Raven维护一组固定的记忆槽，在每个时间步，通过学习的输入依赖路由机制仅衰减和更新选中的子集，从而避免SWA的基于位置的覆盖和硬性驱逐，同时减少SSM密集状态更新带来的干扰。

Result: 在召回密集型基准测试中，Raven与之前的线性时间基线模型竞争或超越它们，在SSM和SWA均显著退化的长上下文召回任务上表现强劲，并能有效外推到训练长度16倍的上下文，在混合架构中也有类似增益。

Conclusion: Raven通过结合稀疏写入和可控更新，有效缓解了线性时间模型中的记忆干扰与驱逐问题，为长上下文序列建模提供了一种新的高效方案，且具备良好的外推能力。

Abstract: Long-context recall in linear-time sequence models highlights a tradeoff in how they write to memory. State-based linear models, such as state-space models (SSMs) and linear Transformers, write densely, updating the entire state for each newly arrived token, which leads to interference and makes specific past tokens hard to recover. Sliding-window attention (SWA) exhibits the opposite behavior: it writes sparsely by storing explicit token representations, but only within a fixed window, so recall drops once the relevant token is evicted. Interpolating between these models, we introduce Raven, a linear-time sequence model that maintains a fixed set of memory slots and, at each step, decays and updates only a selected subset via learned, input-dependent routing. This lets Raven mitigate SWA's position-based overwriting and hard eviction while reducing interference from dense state updates in SSMs, thereby preserving long-range content much more effectively. Across recall-intensive benchmarks, Raven is competitive with or outperforms prior linear-time baselines, achieving strong long-context recall where both SWA and SSMs sharply degrade. It remains effective when extrapolating to context lengths as large as 16x its training length, with similar gains in hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [167] [Rethinking Likelihood distributions: Student's t Likelihood Boosts Bayesian Neural Network Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25376)
*Pei-Hsuan Hsia,Lars H. Heyen,Arvid Weyrauch,Markus Goetz,Achim Streit,Sebastian Krumscheid,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯神经网络中变分推断的似然分布选择问题，通过实验比较发现，学生t分布作为似然分布相比常用的高斯分布能带来更好的预测性能，且与数据分布和网络架构无关，有时还能缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分推断是贝叶斯神经网络中广泛使用的不确定性建模框架，通常假设先验、近似后验和似然分布均为高斯分布。然而已有研究表明这一假设可能不成立，存在模型误设风险。虽然已有工作探索了先验分布的替代选择，但似然分布的选择影响尚未被研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究非高斯似然分布能否优于常用的高斯似然分布。

Method: 在标准多层感知机上，比较多种似然分布假设（如偏态分布、重尾分布等）在人工数据集和真实数据集上的回归任务表现，重点关注学生t分布与高斯分布的对比。

Result: 学生t分布作为似然分布相比高斯似然分布能够获得更好的预测性能，这一结论独立于数据分布和MLP架构（深度和宽度）。在某些情况下，学生t分布还能缩短训练时间，同时实现简单。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯神经网络中，采用学生t分布替代高斯分布作为似然分布可以提升预测性能，且不影响实现的简便性，有时还能加速训练。

Abstract: In Bayesian neural networks (BNNs), variational inference is a widely adopted framework for modeling uncertainty in a distributional way, with the evidence lower bound (ELBO) serving as the standard objective function. Several distributions contribute to the ELBO loss, such as the prior, approximated posterior, and likelihood distribution. Typically, these distributions are all approximated by a Gaussian distribution, since it is easy to compute, allows for reparameterized gradients, and provides a closed-form loss for training. However, several works have highlighted that this assumption may not generally hold, posing the risk of model misspecification. Alternative distributions have been proposed for the prior specifically, while the effect of distribution choice on the likelihood distribution remains unexplored. In this work, our aim is to close this gap by investigating whether alternative assumptions for the likelihood distribution can outperform the commonly used Gaussian. We compare several likelihood distribution assumptions, such as skewed or heavy-tailed, across regression tasks on both artificial and real-world datasets using standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Our findings demonstrate that Student's t yields better predictive performance than a Gaussian likelihood distribution, independent of the data distribution and MLP architecture (depth and width). In some cases, Student's t can also lead to shorter training times, while still being easy to implement.

</details>


### [168] [Learned, Relied Upon, or Necessary? Separating Checkpoint Dependence from Task-Level Value in Sheaf GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25387)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑将 sheaf 图神经网络中的学习限制映射视为模型发现有用边几何证据的常见做法，提出通过检查点依赖和协议相对替换两种估计量来区分映射的依赖性与任务价值，并在多个基准上证明学习映射在重新训练后往往可以被替代，因此并非不可或缺的边几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将学习到的限制映射视为模型发现了有用的边几何结构，但仅凭参数移动或事后消融无法证明该结论，因为这类分析只能展示单个检查点的组织方式，无法回答在模型其余部分适应后学习到的传输是否仍然有帮助。本文旨在严格区分这些主张，并提供评估框架。

Method: 提出两个估计量：检查点依赖（对固定预测器的映射进行干预）和协议相对替换（重新训练匹配的家族以移除映射容量、边变异或持久边分配）。通过任务零定理证明两者可能分歧：标签仅标识传输的分类器方向，留下 d×d 映射中 d^2-d 个不可见自由度。构建精确框架模型给出依赖变为不可替换任务价值的边界。采用仅标签训练实现预测的分离，并在公开 NSD、DNSD 和有向 sheaf 神经网络（DSNN）实现上进行审计。

Result: 所有五个 DNSD 基准均表现出固定检查点依赖。重新训练后，在四个基准上破坏分配或共享映射的控制完全恢复了完整性能；Roman-Empire 在十个官方划分上比连续重采样分配保持 0.0675 的优势，比参数匹配的共享映射保持 0.0391 的优势。这表明学习映射可以支配已拟合的计算，但不构成不可或缺的边几何。

Conclusion: 学习映射可以主导已训练的计算，但并非一定是不可或缺的边几何。关于学习传输的主张应将检查点干预与匹配的重新训练相结合。

Abstract: Learned restriction maps in sheaf graph neural networks are often treated as proof that the model has discovered useful edge geometry. That conclusion does not follow from parameter movement or from a post-hoc ablation: both can show how one checkpoint is organized while leaving open whether learned transport still helps after the rest of the model adapts. We separate these claims with two estimands. Checkpoint reliance intervenes on the maps of a fixed predictor; protocol-relative replacement retrains matched families that remove map capacity, edge variation, or persistent edge assignment. A task-null theorem shows why the claims can diverge: labels identify only the transported classifier directions, leaving $d^2-d$ invisible degrees of freedom in every full $d\times d$ map. An exact frame model then gives the boundary at which reliance becomes unreplaced task value. Label-only training realizes the predicted separation, while audits of public NSD, DNSD, and Directed Sheaf Neural Network (DSNN) implementations recover both replaceable and unreplaced transport regimes on real graphs. All five DNSD benchmarks exhibit fixed-checkpoint reliance. After retraining, assignment-breaking or shared-map controls recover Full performance on four; Roman-Empire retains a $.0675$ advantage over continually resampled assignment and a $.0391$ advantage over a parameter-matched shared map across ten official splits. Thus, a learned map can govern a fitted computation without constituting indispensable edge geometry. Claims of learned transport should pair checkpoint interventions with matched retraining.

</details>


### [169] [TWICE: Two-Clock, Two-Window Learning for Long-Horizon Conversion Prediction in Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25404)
*Kaiyuan Li,Kun Wang,Zhongbo Wang,Teng Sha,Ming Yan,Yanhua Cheng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWICE is a framework for long-horizon post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction under delayed feedback in online advertising. It factorizes CVR into a target-window conversion probability and a grouped elapsed-delay cumulative distribution function (CDF), leveraging two complementary supervision signals: the click clock (timely but partial status) and the conversion clock (delayed but complete arrival data). A novel arrival-conditioned likelihood uses predicted CVR mass to handle cohort mixture. Experiments show significant revenue and conversion gains in Kwai's advertising system, leading to full deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon conversion prediction suffers from delayed feedback, creating a two-clock (click vs. conversion) and two-window (base observation vs. target conversion) problem. The click clock provides timely but partially observed supervision, while the conversion clock reveals long-tail delays but is confounded by historical click cohorts with varying traffic and conversion rates. Existing methods struggle to combine these asynchronous signals and produce monotonic predictions for arbitrary horizons up to the target window.

Method: TWICE factorizes long-horizon post-click CVR into a target-window conversion probability (trained on click-clock data via a current-status likelihood over the base observation window) and a grouped elapsed-delay CDF (trained on newly arrived conversions from the conversion clock). To address cohort mixture, the delay model uses an arrival-conditioned likelihood that weights each arrival-time slice by the fixed predicted CVR (pCVR) mass of its historical click cohort, accounting for differences in traffic volume and conversion propensity. The resulting aggregate records are self-contained, and a single learned CDF produces monotone predictions for any horizon up to the target window without historical lookup or convolution.

Result: Experiments on a public benchmark and an industrial advertising dataset demonstrate effectiveness. In an online A/B test in Kwai's advertising system, TWICE increased expected revenue by 2.486%, revenue by 1.858%, and conversions by 2.061% compared to the baseline. It was subsequently deployed to full traffic.

Conclusion: TWICE effectively solves the two-clock, two-window delayed feedback problem for long-horizon CVR prediction, achieving significant online gains and enabling monotone horizon-flexible predictions. The framework is self-contained and suitable for large-scale deployment.

Abstract: Long-horizon conversion prediction under delayed feedback creates a two-clock, two-window learning problem in online advertising. A short base observation window releases recent clicks on the click clock before their outcomes mature, whereas conversions continue to arrive on the conversion clock throughout a longer target conversion window. The click clock provides timely but partially observed status supervision. The conversion clock reveals long-tail delays, but the delay composition within an arrival-time slice is weighted by historical click cohorts with different traffic volumes and target-window conversion rates.
  We present TWICE, a framework that factorizes long-horizon post-click conversion rate (CVR) into a target-window conversion probability and a grouped elapsed-delay cumulative distribution function (CDF). The two clocks provide complementary supervision. Click-clock records train the target-window CVR head through a current-status likelihood over the base observation window. Newly arrived conversions train the delay model on the conversion clock. To account for the cohort mixture, TWICE uses fixed click-time predicted CVR (pCVR) mass as cohort exposure in an arrival-conditioned likelihood. This accounts for differences in cohort traffic and conversion propensity. The resulting aggregate records are self-contained. A single learned CDF produces monotone predictions for all requested horizons up to the target conversion window. Serving requires neither historical lookup nor convolution. Experiments on a public benchmark and an industrial advertising dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of TWICE. In an online A/B test in Kwai's advertising system, TWICE increased expected revenue, revenue, and conversions by 2.486%, 1.858%, and 2.061%, respectively. It was subsequently deployed to full traffic.

</details>


### [170] [SPARC Segmentation to Prediction via Affine Regression and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25413)
*Shivani,Subhayan Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对B2B电子商务中交易倾向预测的类别不平衡问题，提出一种生产部署的框架，用DiCE反事实生成替代SMOTE过采样以提升少数类样本分布保真度，并结合PyPARC分段仿射分类器输出校准概率，在1:9不平衡比的真实数据上较SMOTE基线提升9.2个百分点（阈值0.8时精度达93.1%），实现高精度营销定向与客户激活提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B2B交易倾向预测面临组织实体异质性采购行为的独特挑战，破坏了SMOTE关于类内特征同质性的隐含假设——B2B买家呈现多模态采购周期，使得少数类样本间的线性插值在结构上无效，生成的合成数据无法代表真实采购行为。现有SMOTE方法在B2B场景下分布保真度不足，亟需能捕获B2B采购复杂性的数据增强与可解释分类方法。

Method: 提出双贡献框架：(1) 用多样化反事实解释(DiCE)生成合成少数类样本，替代传统SMOTE，通过定量邻近性分析和UMAP聚类可视化验证其具有更优分布保真度；(2) 改编PyPARC分段仿射分类框架生成校准的倾向概率，实现客户可解释分群为可操作风险层级。在两年期大型B2B电商平台数据（1:9不平衡比）上评估。

Result: 在决策阈值0.8时达到93.1%精度，相比同阈值下SMOTE基线(83.9%)提升9.2个百分点；在阈值0.7时(66.04%)提升26.1个百分点，在所有操作点均表现一致优越性。框架能够支持高精度营销活动，显著提升客户激活率与投资回报率。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了B2B场景下类别不平衡与采购行为异质性问题，通过DiCE生成高质量少数类样本和PyPARC提供校准可解释概率，在生产环境中实现了显著优于SMOTE的倾向预测性能，为高精度定向营销提供了可靠技术支撑。

Abstract: Transaction propensity prediction in B2B e commerce presents unique challenges distinct from B2C contexts, primarily due to the heterogeneous procurement behaviors of organizational entities, which violate SMOTE's implicit assumption of within class feature homogeneity. Specifically, B2B buyers exhibit multi modal procurement cycles that render linear interpolation between minority class samples structurally invalid, producing synthetic data that does not represent real purchasing behavior. This paper introduces a production deployed propensity modeling framework designed to address these complexities through two primary contributions. First, we replace conventional SMOTE based augmentation with a synthetic data generation approach leveraging Diverse Counterfactual Explanations (DiCE). This method produces minority class samples with superior distributional fidelity compared to SMOTE, as validated through quantitative proximity analysis and UMAP cluster visualization. Second, we adapt the PyPARC piecewise affine classification framework to generate calibrated propensity probabilities, facilitating the interpretable segmentation of customers into actionable risk tiers. Evaluated on two years of longitudinal data from a large scale B2B e commerce platform with a 1 to 9 class imbalance ratio, the proposed architecture achieves 93.1% precision at a decision threshold of 0.8, a 9.2 percentage point improvement over SMOTE based baselines at the same threshold (83.9%), and a 26.1 point improvement over SMOTE at threshold 0.7 (66.04%), demonstrating consistent superiority across operating points. These results demonstrate the framework's efficacy in enabling high precision marketing campaigns with significant improvements in customer activation and return on investment.

</details>


### [171] [PIcsC: Partitioning-Induced Covariate Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25441)
*Behraj Khan,Behroz Mirza,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Fisher信息矩阵的协变量偏移校正框架PIcsC，通过分区局部梯度统计量近似分区散度并作为正则化项，统一适用于集中式（批处理/交叉验证）和分布式（联邦学习）场景，在40+数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据分区间的协变量偏移导致模型选择偏误和参数估计偏差，影响交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习的效果。现有方法缺乏统一且高效的校正机制。

Method: 利用Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)近似分区与参考分布之间的散度，将所得统计量作为优化过程中的正则化项；引入条件适应机制，结合FIM偏移与KL散度检测显著分布偏移，仅在必要时激活正则化。

Result: 在40+数据集上取得一致改进：碎片化批处理/折设置下性能退化降低20%/25%以上；七个联邦学习基准上持续超越FedAvg、FedProx和SCAFFOLD 3-5个百分点，无需客户端个性化。

Conclusion: Fisher信息为缓解集中式和分布式学习中的分区诱导协变量偏移提供了有效且统一的机制。

Abstract: Covariate shift across training-data partitions biases model selection and parameter estimation in cross-validation, lifelong learning, and federated learning. We propose \textit{Partition-Induced Covariate-shift Correction} (\texttt{PIcsC}), a Fisher information-based regularization framework that mitigates distribution mismatch between data partitions and a reference distribution. \texttt{PIcsC} approximates partition divergence using the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) and incorporates the resulting statistic as a regularizer during optimization. The same formulation applies to both centrally partitioned datasets (batches or cross-validation folds) and inherently distributed data (federated clients or decentralized nodes), requiring only partition-local gradient statistics rather than raw data. We further introduce a conditional adaptation mechanism that combines FIM shift with KL divergence to detect significant distribution shifts and activates regularization only when necessary. Experiments on more than 40 datasets demonstrate consistent improvements under both natural and synthetic covariate shift. On fragmented batch and fold settings, \texttt{PIcsC} reduces fragmentation-induced performance degradation by more than 20\% and 25\%, respectively. On seven federated learning benchmarks, it consistently outperforms FedAvg, FedProx, and SCAFFOLD by 3 -5 percentage points without requiring client-specific personalization. These results demonstrate that Fisher information provides an effective and unified mechanism for mitigating partition-induced covariate shift across both centralized and distributed learning.

</details>


### [172] [Bits and Memories: Measuring Verbatim Extraction Across LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25451)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量化（Quantization）会选择性遗忘训练数据中的逐字记忆内容，但其选择性不足以作为隐私防御手段：在最大模型中，4-bit量化仍保留大部分记忆序列，且随模型增大保留比例上升。因此，压缩不能替代隐私保护，提取（extraction）而非成员推理（membership inference）才是实际应关注的隐私风险指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作几乎全部使用成员推理（membership inference）来衡量量化对隐私风险的影响，但公众实际担忧的是模型逐字复现训练数据（verbatim extraction）。作者认为成员推理不能反映真实风险，因此需要直接测量量化对逐字记忆提取的影响。

Method: 使用Pythia模型及其已知记忆的公开序列集合，在全精度到4-bit共五种精度级别、三种模型大小下，追踪逐字提取（verbatim extraction）的变化，同时测量通用能力（困惑度）作为对照。实验采用两种不相关的量化算法和两个评估语料库。

Result: 量化是一种选择性遗忘器：在所有精度和模型规模下，逐字记忆的下降速度均快于通用能力下降；但这种选择性不足以使量化成为隐私防御手段。在最大模型中，4-bit量化仍能重现大部分记忆序列，仅牺牲少量通用能力；且量化后保留的记忆数据比例随模型增大而增长。

Conclusion: 压缩不应被视为移除训练记忆数据的手段；提取（extraction）而非成员推理（membership inference）才是从业者应监控的隐私风险指标。

Abstract: Language models are almost always quantized before they are deployed, and a growing line of work asks whether quantization also lowers their privacy risk. That work measures privacy almost entirely with membership inference. We think this is the wrong thing to measure for the risk that most people actually worry about, namely a model reproducing its training data word for word, and we measure that directly. Using the Pythia models and the public set of sequences each of them is known to have memorized, we track verbatim extraction across five precision levels, from full precision down to four bits, and across three model sizes, while measuring general capability (perplexity) at every point. We find two things. Quantization is a selective forgetter: verbatim memorization falls off faster than capability at every precision and every model size we tried, and this holds under two unrelated quantization algorithms and two evaluation corpora. But the selectivity is not enough to make quantization a privacy defense, which cuts against the optimistic reading of earlier membership-inference results. At the largest model we study, four-bit quantization still reproduces most of the memorized sequences while giving up only a few percent of capability, and the fraction of memorized data that survives quantization grows with model size. We conclude that compression should not be treated as a way to remove memorized training data, and that extraction, not membership inference, is the number practitioners should be watching. All code, sampled evaluation data, and per-configuration results are released.

</details>


### [173] [Emergent Latent-State Computation under Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25459)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Lulu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了序列模型在部分观测噪声环境下如何内部表征潜在随机动力学，以多元随机波动率模型为基准，发现两阶段计算机制：隐藏状态编码潜在波动率信息，输出头映射为预测。Transformer中潜在状态解码出现在特定的架构阶段，长周期下简化为线性投影加L2归一化的显式滤波器。输出头替换表明MSE训练下的性能下降部分源于读取对齐而非表征失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机械可解释性主要针对语言模型和确定性玩具任务，对序列模型如何内部表征含噪声、部分观测的潜在随机动力学了解甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过可控的多元随机波动率场景提供基准。

Method: 在多元随机波动率设置中，模型仅观测收益率，而真实潜在波动率状态已知。使用多种架构、损失函数和输出头，分析隐藏表示对下一潜在波动率状态的编码能力，以及输出头如何将表示映射为平方收益率预测。特别在Transformer中考察潜在状态可解码性出现的架构阶段，并探究长周期下的简化滤波机制。

Result: 发现两阶段计算：隐藏表示编码大量关于下一潜在波动率状态的信息，输出头将其映射为预测。Transformer中潜在状态可解码性出现在可识别的架构阶段，位置取决于波动率周期。长周期下计算简化为显式潜在状态滤波器（线性投影+L2归一化）。输出头替换显示MSE训练下的性能下降部分源于读取对齐问题而非表征失败。

Conclusion: 随机波动率模型为在噪声潜在动力学和部分可观测性下进行机械可解释性研究提供了有用基准，揭示了序列模型内部计算的组织原则和局限性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks. Much less is known about how sequence models internally represent latent stochastic dynamics under noisy, partially observed observations. We study this question in a controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting, where models observe only returns while the ground-truth latent volatility state is known to the researcher. This setting provides a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under partial observability: the latent state is hidden from the model but directly available for evaluation. Across architectures, losses, and output heads, we find evidence for a two-stage computation. Hidden representations encode substantial information about the next latent volatility state, and the output head maps this representation to squared return forecasts. Furthermore, in Transformers, latent-state decodability emerges at identifiable architectural stages whose location depends on the volatility period. In long-cycle regimes, this computation simplifies into an explicit latent-state filter consisting of a learned linear projection followed by $\ell^2$ normalization. Output-head replacement further shows that part of the degradation under noisy MSE training arises from readout misalignment rather than representation failure. These results suggest that stochastic volatility models provide a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under noisy latent dynamics and partial observability.

</details>


### [174] [Data-Dependent Regret and Polyak Corrections for Constrained Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25480)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对约束在线凸优化问题，在标准OGD-Polyak方法的基础上，通过引入数据依赖的梯度累积和Polyak校正项，推导出更紧的遗憾界，并提出了自适应步长版本AdaOGD-PFS，实验表明遗憾界可改进38–43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束在线凸优化方法（如OGD+Polyak可行性步骤）虽能达到O(√T)遗憾，但其分析基于最坏情况，忽略了实际数据中的梯度变化和投影带来的修正效应，导致界限不够紧凑。作者希望利用实际观测到的梯度累积和投影位移来收紧遗憾界。

Method: 保留标准最坏情况分析中省略的两个量：一是实际梯度范数平方和G_T=∑_t‖∇f_t(x_t)‖²，二是非负的Polyak校正P_T（衡量可行性投影导致的累计平方位移）。基于此重新分析遗憾界，得到改进项Δ_T=(η/2)(G_f²T - G_T)+P_T/(2η)。进一步提出自适应步长方法AdaOGD-PFS，实现O(√G_T)遗憾并保持每轮可行性。

Result: 推导了数据依赖的遗憾界，改进项Δ_T始终非负。在球约束和半空间约束问题上的实验显示，遗憾界改进幅度达38%–43%，数据依赖的梯度项和Polyak校正项均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 数据依赖分析能有效收紧约束在线凸优化的遗憾界，自适应方法AdaOGD-PFS在保持可行性的同时实现了更优的遗憾表现，为安全关键应用提供了更高效的理论与实践工具。

Abstract: Constrained online convex optimization requires minimizing regret against adversarial convex costs while satisfying a convex constraint at every round, as needed in safety-critical applications. A computationally efficient method combines online gradient descent with a Polyak feasibility step, using one constraint evaluation and one subgradient per round. Although this method achieves O(sqrt(T)) regret with per-round feasibility, we derive a tighter, data-dependent analysis by retaining two quantities omitted by the standard worst-case argument. First, we replace the gradient envelope G_f^2 T with the observed accumulation G_T = sum_t ||grad f_t(x_t)||^2. Second, we identify a nonnegative Polyak correction P_T that measures the cumulative squared displacement caused by feasibility projections and enters the regret bound with a negative sign. The resulting improvement, Delta_T = (eta/2)(G_f^2 T - G_T) + P_T/(2 eta), is always nonnegative. We further propose AdaOGD-PFS, an adaptive-step-size method that achieves O(sqrt(G_T)) regret while preserving per-round feasibility. Experiments on ball- and halfspace-constrained problems improve the regret bound by 38 to 43 percent, with both data-dependent gradients and Polyak corrections contributing substantially.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum Speedups for Stochastic Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25492)
*Bin Luo,Chengchang Liu,Jonathan Allcock,Shengyu Zhang,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究重尾梯度噪声下的随机优化问题。首先提出一种针对多变量重尾随机变量的新型量子均值估计器，在低维区域实现了比最优经典估计器更低的查询复杂度。进一步通过广义多层蒙特卡罗技术开发了无偏的量子均值估计器。基于这些估计器，提出了量子归一化随机梯度下降法（QNSGD）和非凸优化，以及量子投影随机梯度下降法（QPSGD）用于凸优化，在低维区域获得了比经典下界更优的查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾梯度噪声在实际机器学习中常见（如梯度裁剪、鲁棒优化），经典随机优化在重尾噪声下的查询复杂度下界已被证明。量子计算可能提供加速，但如何设计量子算法在重尾噪声下实现更优的查询复杂度是核心问题。

Method: 1. 提出一种量子均值估计器，利用量子振幅估计等技术，在低维区域以更少查询估计重尾随机向量的均值。2. 结合广义多层蒙特卡罗方法得到无偏量子均值估计器。3. 分别针对非凸和凸目标函数，设计量子归一化SGD（QNSGD）和量子投影SGD（QPSGD），将量子均值估计器与随机优化框架结合。

Result: 非凸问题：QNSGD达到Õ(√d ε^{-(5p-4)/(2p-2)})查询复杂度，优于经典下界Ω(ε^{-(3p-2)/(p-1)})。凸问题：QPSGD达到Õ(√d ε^{-(3p-2)/(2p-2)} + ε^{-2})查询复杂度，优于经典下界Ω(ε^{-p/(p-1)})。量子均值估计器本身在低维区域（d为常数）达到最优（对数因子内）。

Conclusion: 量子算法在重尾噪声随机优化中可获得显著的查询复杂度提升，尤其在低维区域；量子均值估计器是最优的；维度依赖下界表明低维区域中维度影响不可避免。

Abstract: We study stochastic optimization with heavy-tailed gradient noise. We first propose a novel quantum mean estimator for multivariate heavy-tailed random variables that achieves lower query complexity than optimal classical estimators in the low-dimensional regime. We further develop an unbiased quantum mean estimator by applying a generalized multi-level Monte Carlo technique. We prove quantum lower bounds showing that, when the dimension $d$ of the random vector is small and can be viewed as a constant, our quantum estimators are optimal up to logarithmic factors. We further derive stronger dimension-dependent lower bounds for tail index $p>4/3$, showing that a nontrivial dependence on the dimension is unavoidable in the low-dimensional regime. Based on these estimators, we propose a quantum normalized stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QNSGD}$), which finds an $ε$-stationary point using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}}\big)$ queries to the quantum stochastic gradient oracle. For a convex objective function, we propose a quantum projected stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QPSGD}$), which computes a solution with $ε$-optimal solution using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+ε^{-2}\big)$ queries in expectation. These sharper bounds improve upon the classical lower bounds $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}}\big)$ for nonconvex problems and $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}\big)$ for convex problems in the low-dimensional regimes $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}$ and $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{2-p}{p-1}}$, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection via Cumulative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25502)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反后门核心集选择的训练时防御方法，通过构建良性核心集来训练无后门模型，利用累积熵作为选择标准，并结合每轮遗忘所选样本的策略，有效分离有毒样本，在保持自然性能的同时稳定抵御后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时防御方法需要从被污染的训练数据中隔离出良性子集来训练无后门模型，但如何高效选择良性样本仍具挑战。本文将此问题形式化为核心集选择问题，旨在利用有毒样本预测不确定性低且出现频率低的特点，通过累积熵跟踪学习动态，选择高信息量的良性样本。

Method: 将防御策略形式化为反后门核心集选择问题，使用累积熵作为选择标准，跟踪训练样本的学习动态以挑选高信息量的良性样本；同时每轮遗忘所选样本，增强良性样本与有毒样本的可分离性，最终构建良性核心集用于训练无后门模型。

Result: 该方法稳定地缓解了后门攻击，且对自然性能的影响可忽略不计。与以往牺牲自然准确率或无法防御某些攻击的方法不同，本方法在各种攻击下均表现一致。

Conclusion: 反后门核心集选择是一种有效的训练时防御手段，通过累积熵和遗忘机制能够可靠地构建良性核心集，在不损害自然性能的前提下抵御后门攻击。

Abstract: Recent training-time defenses against neural backdoors isolate a benign subset from poisoned training data, to learn a backdoor-free model from it. In this paper, we formulate this defense strategy as a coreset selection problem, giving rise to so-called "Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection." Since poisonous samples have (a) lower prediction uncertainty and are (b) less frequent than benign samples, coreset selection naturally focuses more on samples associated with benign functionality than the backdoor functionality. We use the Cumulative Entropy as selection criterion to further facilitate this effect. The metric tracks the learning dynamics of training samples and allowing us to select benign samples with high informativeness for the coreset. Additionally, we unlearn the chosen samples in each epoch to facilitate the separability between benign and poisonous samples. Together, this yields an exceptionally effective training-time defense that constructs a benign coreset to train a backdoor-free model. Unlike prior defenses that compromise natural accuracy and fail against certain attacks, our method mitigates backdooring attacks consistently with a negligible impact on natural performance.

</details>


### [177] [AMPBench-MT: A Homology-Controlled Benchmark for Antimicrobial Peptide Potency, Spectrum, and Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25518)
*Ziheng Zhou,Huiyu Luo,Xiaohu Zhu,Nan Wang,Xuebiao Qin,Chaoyan Zhang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMPBench-MT 是一个保留来源的抗菌肽基准，统一了二元识别、物种条件 pMIC 回归以及 endpoint 特异性效力与安全性指标，证明高二元识别性能并不保证 assay 端点行为，并呼吁评测从识别排行榜转向端点感知证据审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准分别覆盖二元识别、多标签注释、检测回归或肽模型比较，但未能将 AMP 识别、物种条件效力、抗菌谱、安全性代理端点及跨端点行为整合到一个序列同源性受控的协议中。

Method: 构建 AMPBench-MT，标准化规范肽记录，组织成二元识别、物种条件 pMIC 回归、以及 endpoint 特异性效力与安全性读数。在 161 个 endpoint 特异性模型评估上比较多种模型（包括蛋白质语言模型嵌入、图模型和经典回归器）。

Result: 高二元识别性能不可靠地指示检测端点行为；冻结的蛋白质语言模型嵌入在 pMIC 错误中领先，而图和经典回归器表现接近；谱标签显示 PR 指标在负样本稀缺时具有误导性，而低毒性、HC50 溶血和选择性虽信号较小但更面向检测。

Conclusion: AMP 评估应从识别排行榜转向端点感知证据审计，AMPBench-MT 提供了实现这一转变的标准化平台。

Abstract: Computational AMP discovery is often evaluated through AMP/non-AMP recognition, yet follow-up decisions depend on assay-derived evidence such as target-species potency, hemolysis, toxicity, and selectivity. Existing AMP and peptide benchmarks cover binary recognition, multilabel annotation, assay regression, or broader peptide-model comparison, but they do not jointly place AMP recognition, species-conditioned potency, spectrum, safety-facing proxy endpoints, and cross-endpoint behavior within one sequence-homology-controlled protocol. To address this problem, we introduce AMPBench-MT, a provenance-preserving benchmark that standardizes canonical peptide records and organizes them into binary recognition, species-conditioned pMIC regression, and endpoint-specific potency and safety-facing readouts. Across 161 endpoint-specific model evaluations, high binary performance does not reliably indicate assay-endpoint behavior. Frozen protein-language-model embeddings form the leading pMIC error cluster, while graph and classical regressors remain close. Spectrum labels further reveal that PR-oriented metrics can be misleading under scarce observed negatives, whereas low-toxicity, HC50 hemolysis, and selectivity expose smaller but more assay-facing signals. AMPBench-MT shows that AMP evaluation should move beyond recognition leaderboards toward endpoint-aware evidence auditing. Our proposed benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT.

</details>


### [178] [Multi-Scale Structural Features for Continual, Comprehensible Visual Recognition in a Developmental Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25531)
*Zeki Doruk Erden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示，用于基于发育拓扑的、无需梯度、无需回放的持续学习框架。在类增量MNIST上，该方法匹配甚至超越基于回放和正则化的基线，同时完全不存储历史数据，并保留已学类别知识，学习到的表示仍具有人类可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代机器学习难以实现持续学习、重用先前知识和提供可理解的内部结构。现有的基于发育拓扑的无梯度持续学习框架在处理视觉输入时，其特征表示表达能力有限，导致识别准确率受限。

Method: 引入一种新的视觉特征表示，在多个尺度上编码形状结构，捕获边缘和轮廓特征及其空间关系，并将其融入网络精化学习过程；同时改进学习动力学和用于从学习模型中预测的读出机制。

Result: 在类增量MNIST上，新表示大幅提升准确率，在同等存储条件下匹配或超越基于回放和正则化的基线，且不存储任何历史数据。早期学习的类别在引入新类别时被保留，无破坏性适应；基线方法会在每个类别学习周期内放弃刚学的类别然后重新学习，而本方法不会。

Conclusion: 该方法逐样本整合信息，同时可证明地保留对先前输入的响应。其意义在于学习方式本身——持续学习无需回放缓冲区或预定义任务边界，学习到的表示保持人类可解释性。

Abstract: Contemporary machine learning struggles to learn continually, reuse prior knowledge, and expose a comprehensible internal structure. A recently proposed developmental, gradient-free learning framework addresses these limitations by learning a discrete, topological model of its inputs through local variation and selection, yielding an inherent continual-learning guarantee: new observations refine existing structure without overwriting past knowledge, and without replay buffers or predefined task boundaries. Its extension to visual inputs demonstrated this principle on shape recognition, but relied on a feature representation of limited expressivity that capped recognition accuracy. We introduce a new visual feature representation that encodes shape structure across multiple scales, capturing edge and contour features together with their spatial relations, and integrate it with the network-refinement learning process; we further improve the learning dynamics and the read-out used to predict from the learned model. The study targets two-dimensional shape, with class-incremental MNIST as a controlled, interpretable benchmark in which continual-learning behavior can be measured directly. Our approach substantially increases accuracy over the prior representation, matching or exceeding replay- and regularisation-based baselines at comparable storage while storing no past data, and preserves the framework's defining behavior: earlier-learned classes are retained as new ones are introduced, with no destructive adaptation, and the learned representations remain human-interpretable. What separates the methods is retention: the baselines surrender most of a just-trained class within its own cycle and relearn it afterwards, which ours does not. The significance lies in the manner of learning. The system integrates information one sample at a time while provably preserving its responses to...

</details>


### [179] [Mind the Missing Split: Resolving Feature Heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25538)
*Mohammad Tajabadi,Dominik Heider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对群学习（Swarm Learning）中随机森林在部分重叠特征空间下的特征异构问题，提出了多种推理时策略（确定性和概率性）来处理缺失分裂，无需将训练限制在特征交集上。在九个数据集上验证了所提方法优于交集基线和本地训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在水平群学习中，各站点数据集通常假设共享相同特征集，但实际应用中站点间特征往往部分重叠（如测量方法、协议、可用协变量不同），导致随机森林全局模型推理时遇到本地缺失特征的分裂节点而无法处理，迫使组织舍弃站点特有特征，影响了模型利用全部特征的能力。

Method: 提出了多种推理时策略来解决缺失分裂问题，包括确定性方法和概率性方法。这些策略在推理阶段处理随机森林中遇到的特征缺失情况，无需在训练时强制特征对齐。

Result: 在九个数据集上的实验表明，所提出的推理时策略在多种场景下均优于特征交集基线方法以及各站点独立训练的本地模型。

Conclusion: 本文证明在群学习框架下，通过推理时策略可以有效处理特征异构问题，使随机森林能够利用各站点的全部特征而无需舍弃特有变量，从而提升模型性能。

Abstract: Swarm Learning is a decentralized collaborative learning mechanism that allows multiple organizations to train a shared model without central coordination or direct data sharing. In typical horizontal Swarm Learning, datasets across sites are usually assumed to share the same feature set. However, in real-world applications, sites often have partially overlapping features because measurements, protocols, and available covariates differ across sites. This feature heterogeneity creates a practical issue for machine learning algorithms such as Random Forests. Specifically, when decision trees are pooled into a global Random Forest, inference at a given site can become ill-defined if a traversal encounters a split on a feature that is not available locally, often forcing organizations to discard site-specific variables upfront. In this paper, we address feature heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests under partially overlapping feature spaces. We propose several deterministic and probabilistic inference-time strategies that resolve such missing splits without restricting training to the intersection of features. We evaluate the methods on nine datasets and demonstrate that they outperform both the intersection baseline and locally trained models across a broad range of scenarios.

</details>


### [180] [OrthKD: Extracting Generalized Clinical Knowledge from Heterogeneous Teachers for Lightweight Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25545)
*Yi Xu,Cheng Chen,Mufan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes OrthKD, a selective-trust knowledge distillation framework for diabetic retinopathy (DR) screening on edge devices. It uses a strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3) for full supervision, a weaker ViT teacher (Swin-Base) for feature-only distillation, and enforces orthogonality between teacher-specific student projections to leverage complementary information while avoiding misleading logits. A 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student achieves 0.885 QWK on EyePACS and improves zero-shot Messidor-2 from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, demonstrating practical DR screening on resource-constrained devices.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deploying DR screening in primary care requires edge-efficient models that remain accurate under domain shift. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation is a natural compression strategy, but existing methods assume all teachers are equally trustworthy, which fails when a weaker Transformer teacher's logits can mislead the student despite being complementary to a strong CNN teacher. This motivates a selective-trust distillation approach that handles heterogeneous teachers appropriately.

Method: OrthKD transfers full supervision (logit-level) from the strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3), uses feature-only distillation from the weaker ViT teacher (Swin-Base), and enforces orthogonality between the teacher-specific student projections. This design preserves local lesion precision (from CNN), injects global structural context (from ViT), and improves robustness to distribution shift.

Result: On 132,049 retinal images, a 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student reaches 0.885 QWK on EyePACS (vs. 0.876 for the CNN teacher and 0.830 for the ViT teacher), improves zero-shot Messidor-2 performance from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, and achieves strong referral AUC and calibration.

Conclusion: Selectively distilling heterogeneous teachers (strong CNN and weaker ViT) with orthogonal projections enables practical, accurate, and robust DR screening on resource-constrained devices, demonstrating that not all teachers need equal trust in multi-teacher KD.

Abstract: Deploying diabetic retinopathy (DR) screening models in primary care requires edge-efficient systems that remain accurate, safe, and reliable under domain shift. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation (KD) is a natural compression strategy, but existing approaches largely assume that all teachers provide equally trustworthy supervision. In our setting, this assumption fails: a strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3, 0.876 QWK) and a weaker Transformer teacher (Swin-Base, 0.830 QWK) are complementary, yet the Transformer's logits can still mislead the student. We therefore propose OrthKD, a selective-trust distillation framework that transfers full supervision from the strong CNN, uses feature-only distillation from the weak ViT, and enforces orthogonality between teacher-specific student projections to encourage complementary rather than redundant evidence. This design preserves local lesion precision, injects global structural context, and improves robustness to distribution shift. On 132,049 retinal images, a 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student reaches 0.885 QWK on EyePACS and improves zero-shot Messidor-2 performance from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, while also achieving strong referral AUC and calibration. These results show that selectively distilling heterogeneous teachers can enable practical DR screening on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [181] [Physics-Informed Broad Learning System: An Efficient Backpropagation-Free Framework for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25608)
*Pinki Khatun,M. Sajid,Abhinav Jha,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的广度学习系统（PI-BLS），通过将物理约束嵌入线性输出层优化问题，利用伪逆求解最小二乘解，避免了非线性梯度训练，从而显著提升训练效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）虽然能通过嵌入物理定律求解偏微分方程，但依赖昂贵的梯度优化和深层架构，导致训练缓慢、计算成本高且可扩展性有限。

Method: 提出PI-BLS框架，将微分算子及初始/边界条件直接嵌入线性输出层优化问题，通过伪逆求解确定性最小二乘解，将学习过程简化为单阶段线性优化，无需迭代反向传播。

Result: 在代表性正向PDE基准测试中，PI-BLS相比传统PINNs在保持竞争力的同时，显著减少了训练时间和模型参数，并取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: PI-BLS为求解PDE提供了一种高效的物理信息学习范式，消除了迭代反向传播，同时保留了物理约束，有望推动该方法在更广泛领域的应用。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding governing physical laws into deep neural networks. However, their reliance on computationally expensive gradient-based optimization and deep architectures often results in slow training, high computational cost, and limited scalability. In this work, we propose a novel physics-informed broad learning system (PI-BLS), the first physics-informed learning framework based on broad RdNNs. The proposed formulation embeds the governing differential operator and the associated initial and boundary constraints directly into a linear output-layer optimization problem, thereby replacing nonlinear gradient-based training with a deterministic least-squares solution obtained via the pseudoinverse. Consequently, the entire learning process is reduced to a single linear optimization stage while preserving the underlying physical constraints. As a result, PI-BLS offers an efficient learning paradigm for a physics-informed learning framework for solving PDEs that eliminates iterative backpropagation while preserving the underlying physical constraints. Experimental results on representative forward PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PI-BLS achieves competitive and often superior performance with reduced training time and model parameters compared with conventional PINNs.

</details>


### [182] [Contrastive Representation Learning of Longitudinal Disease Trajectories on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25609)
*Bastian Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比图神经网络的表示学习框架，将纵向临床数据中的多变量疾病轨迹建模为时序图，通过结构感知随机游走引导对比学习，生成保留时序上下文和轨迹拓扑的嵌入，从而实现患者疾病进展模式的鲁棒聚类和潜在结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床数据中疾病轨迹的理解因复杂的时序动态和患者队列异质性而面临挑战，现有方法难以有效建模和利用轨迹中的时序依赖与结构信息。

Method: 将多变量疾病轨迹构建为时序图，节点代表患者各时间点的观测，边捕获时序连续性和轨迹间的结构相似性；采用对比图神经网络，结合结构感知随机游走进行对比学习，生成保留时序上下文和轨迹拓扑的嵌入表示。

Result: 学到的表示能够对具有相似疾病进展模式的患者进行鲁棒聚类，并揭示纵向数据中的潜在结构。

Conclusion: 该对比表示学习框架有效建模了疾病轨迹的时序动态，实现了患者分群和潜在模式发现，为纵向临床数据分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding disease trajectories from longitudinal clinical data remains challenging due to complex temporal dynamics and heterogeneous patient cohorts. Here, we present a contrastive representation learning framework that models multivariate disease trajectories as temporal graphs and learns representations using contrastive graph neural networks. Nodes represent patient observations over time, while edges capture temporal continuity and structural similarity between trajectories. Structure-aware random walks guide contrastive learning to generate embeddings that preserve temporal context and trajectory topology. The resulting representations enable robust clustering of patients with similar disease progression patterns and reveal latent structure in longitudinal data.

</details>


### [183] [MemSFT: Mitigating Alignment Tax with an External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25614)
*Jiarui Wang,Xiang Shi,Jiaqi Cao,Rubin Wei,Xiquan Wang,Hao Sun,Jingzhi Wang,Zhiqi Yang,Qipeng Guo,Bowen Zhou,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MemSFT 是一种通过可插拔参数化记忆模块解耦领域专业化与骨干参数更新的方法，能在不牺牲通用能力的前提下提升大语言模型的领域性能，避免了全参数微调带来的灾难性遗忘（即对齐税）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在领域微调时存在对齐税问题，即全参数微调会导致灾难性遗忘，严重损害通用任务性能。现有方法无法有效解耦领域知识与通用能力，亟需一种既能注入领域专长又保持通用能力的技术。

Method: MemSFT 通过可插拔的参数化记忆模块解耦领域专业化与骨干参数更新。该记忆模块学习模仿非参数化检索器在领域数据上的行为，从而记忆本应通过检索获取的知识与模式。训练好的记忆可跨不同规模的 LLM 复用。推理时，一个学习得到的路由器在每个解码步骤动态融合记忆与骨干网络的输出分布，选择性调用领域知识。

Result: 在生物、地球科学和法律三个领域，使用 Qwen3-8B 到 Qwen3-235B-A22B 等模型评估表明，MemSFT 持续提升了领域性能，而通用性能几乎无下降；相比之下，全参数微调在通用任务上遭遇严重遗忘。

Conclusion: MemSFT 提供了一条在参数层面解耦通用模型能力与领域知识的实用路径，使 LLM 在保持通用能力的同时获得新的领域专长。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains often incurs an alignment tax, as fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks can cause catastrophic forgetting and substantially degrade performance on general tasks. We propose MemSFT, which mitigates the alignment tax by decoupling domain specialization from backbone parameter updates through a plug-and-play parametric memory. The memory is trained to imitate the behavior of a non-parametric retriever operating over domain data, thereby memorizing knowledge and patterns that would otherwise be accessed through retrieval. Once trained on a specific domain, the memory can be reused across LLMs of different sizes. During generation, a learned router dynamically fuses the output distributions of the memory and backbone at each decoding step, allowing domain expertise to be invoked selectively. Across biology, geoscience, and law, evaluations with models ranging from Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-235B-A22B show that MemSFT consistently improves domain performance with negligible degradation in general performance, whereas full SFT suffers severe forgetting on general tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate a practical path to decoupling general model capabilities from domain-specific knowledge at the parameter level, thereby equipping LLMs with new specialized capabilities without compromising their general capabilities.

</details>


### [184] [Using Data-Derived Priors to Guide CNN Architecture Design for NIR Chemometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何利用光谱数据集的属性（如熵、本征秩、自相关、小波尺度结构等）作为卷积神经网络设计的经验先验。在25个NIR回归任务上，通过贝叶斯超参数优化和留一数据集验证，发现卷积感受野等超参数与光谱属性存在明确关系，基于这些关系构建的启发性策略能够达到与完整超参数优化相当的性能，表明光谱描述符可以指导浅层NIR CNN模型进入合理的超参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外化学计量学中常用的CNN设计依赖于通用架构规则，但不同光谱数据集在采样、平滑性、冗余度和样本量上存在显著差异。作者希望验证这些数据集属性能否作为CNN设计的经验先验，从而提供更高效且可解释的设计指导，减少对昂贵超参数优化的依赖。

Method: 在25个NIR回归任务上，计算数据集大小、光谱长度与间隔、熵、本征秩、自相关、小波尺度结构等描述符。设计两种可解释的1D-CNN架构（最小单卷积模型和扩展浅层模型），通过五折交叉验证的贝叶斯超参数优化找出近最优配置。从优化结果中提取超参数与数据集属性的关系，转化为热启动启发策略，并直接评估和通过留一数据集验证进行评估。此外还进行了联合预处理与CNN超参数优化的对比实验。

Result: 最清晰的关系涉及卷积感受野：最小CNN中，最优卷积核比例随光谱熵和本征秩增加而减小，随小波能量支持比例增加而增大；学习率随训练集大小增加而降低。直接启发策略和留一数据集启发策略的中位测试RMSE比率分别为0.953和1.017，与超参数优化竞争力相当。扩展CNN中类似关系存在但可迁移性较弱。联合预处理与CNN超参数优化在25个任务中的19个上优于标准化光谱超参数优化，但增益因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 光谱数据集属性可作为实用的CNN设计先验，在目标域具体调优之前就能指导浅层NIR模型进入合理的超参数区域。这种方法在保持性能的同时降低了超参数搜索成本，并且提供了可解释的设计指导。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) for near-infrared (NIR) chemometrics are often designed using generic architectural rules, although spectral datasets differ in sampling, smoothness, redundancy, and sample size. We tested whether these properties can provide empirical priors for CNN design. Across 25 NIR regression tasks, we computed descriptors of dataset size, spectral length and spacing, entropy, intrinsic rank, autocorrelation, and wavelet-scale structure. Two interpretable 1D-CNN scaffolds (a minimal single-convolution model and an extended shallow model with optional branching, dilation, etc) were optimized using five-fold cross-validated Bayesian hyperparameter optimization (HPO). Relationships extracted from near-optimal trials were converted into warm-start heuristics and evaluated directly and through leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) validation. The clearest relationships involved convolutional receptive fields. In the minimal CNN, the preferred kernel fraction decreased with spectral entropy and intrinsic rank, increased with the wavelet energy-support fraction, and the learning rate tended to decrease with training-set size. Direct and LODO heuristics were competitive with HPO, with median test-RMSE ratios of 0.953 and 1.017, respectively. The extended CNN showed similar but less transferable structure across branch usage, dilation, dropout, filter counts, and receptive-field choices. Ten stochastic refits showed seed sensitivity comparable to that of HPO-selected configurations. In a separate experiment, joint preprocessing and CNN HPO outperformed standardized-spectra HPO in 19 of 25 tasks, although gains were dataset-dependent. These results show that spectral descriptors can provide practical CNN design priors, guiding shallow NIR models toward plausible hyperparameter regions before target-specific tuning

</details>


### [185] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Contextual Deconvolution (CD) is a two-stage demand forecasting estimator that decomposes demand into transient promotional shocks and a smooth baseline using a data-derived kernel operator, enabling catalog-scale deployment via hierarchical pooling. On 30k+ SKUs, CD reduces safety stock and order variance but may increase total cost unless holding costs are sufficiently high; its key accuracy gain is reliability (lowest error dispersion and fewest extreme mis-forecasts) rather than central tendency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy but introduce excess operational volatility, inflating safety stock and amplifying the Bullwhip effect. The paper aims to address this by reframing demand sensing as a convex decomposition that separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline.

Method: Contextual Deconvolution (CD) is a two-stage estimator: (1) a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline via convex decomposition; (2) hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived and reduces to identity where promotional response is impulsive, contributing only where genuine multi-day carryover exists.

Result: On 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes. Total cost is reduced only when holding costs exceed ~20% of stockout costs (95% CI [17%,25%]); otherwise it provides operational stability and inventory-capital benefits. Accuracy-wise, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by >200% on 0.8% of SKUs versus 9.9–20.6% for baselines, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws.

Conclusion: The learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably. CD is not a universal expected-cost minimizer; its primary contribution lies in reliability (low error dispersion) and operational-stability/inventory-capital benefits, with cost reduction contingent on the relative magnitude of holding vs. stockout costs.

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [186] [A Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Thermal Ranking of Low-Cost Wall Materials in Hot-Dry Climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25668)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan,Fahim Raees,Ubaida Fatima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合有限差分法（FDM）与物理信息神经算子（PINO）的两阶段计算框架，用于评估五种低成本本土墙体材料（泥砖、粘土-秸秆土坯、石灰-竹子复合板、烧结粘土砖、石灰-泥复合体）在炎热干燥气候下的热性能。PINO以仅150个FDM样本达到与两倍数据量纯数据FNO相当的精度，且准确复现FDM材料排序；在标准炎热干燥夏季条件下，粘土-秸秆土坯具有最佳性价比；气候扫描发现，在低于室外气温条件下，材料排序反转，导电性强的烧结粘土砖胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 炎热干燥气候（夏季气温常>45°C）下，低收入农村住房需用低成本本土建材减少墙体热穿透以改善室内热舒适，但缺乏系统量化比较这些材料热性能的方法。

Method: 两阶段计算框架：阶段一，采用验证过的Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解一维瞬态热方程（含Robin边界条件、逐日太阳辐射及室外气温驱动），通过拉丁超立方采样生成1500个周期日解（九维参数空间）；阶段二，以傅里叶神经算子为骨干构建物理信息神经算子，学习从材料参数到温度场T(x,t)的算子，强制满足数据保真度和PDE一致性。

Result: PINO的相对L2场误差5.14e-4，峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差0.201 K，准确保持FDM材料排序；PINO仅用150个FDM样本即可达到数据纯FNO用300个样本的性能，证明物理损失在数据稀缺时最有价值；周期日公式复现ISO 13786时间滞后与衰减因子，误差分别在0.99 h和0.010以内；标准条件下粘土-秸秆土坯性价比最优；气候扫描发现临界点：当室外条件低于环境温度时，排序反转，导电性好的烧结粘土砖成为最佳。

Conclusion: 该框架为炎热干燥地区灾后重建中的材料选择提供了基于证据的量化支持，揭示了不同气候工况下的材料适用性边界（热排除与热拒绝模式），验证了物理信息神经网络在工程热分析中数据高效性。

Abstract: Identifying cost-effective indigenous building materials that minimise heat penetration through walls is critical for indoor thermal comfort in low-income rural housing in hot-dry climates, where summer temperatures routinely exceed 45 C. We present a two-stage computational framework for thermal ranking of five low-cost indigenous wall materials: mud brick, clay-straw adobe, lime-stabilised bamboo panel, fired clay brick, and lime-mud composite. First, a validated Crank-Nicolson finite difference method (FDM) solves the one-dimensional transient heat equation with Robin boundary conditions under diurnal solar and outdoor air-temperature forcing, generating 1500 periodic-day solutions across a nine-dimensional parameter space by Latin Hypercube sampling. Second, a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone learns the parameter-to-solution operator mu -> T(x,t), enforcing both data fidelity and PDE consistency. The trained PINO attains a relative L2 field error of 5.14e-4 and a 0.201 K mean absolute error on the peak inner surface temperature, preserving the FDM material ranking exactly; PINO trained on 150 FDM samples matches a data-only FNO trained on twice as many, so the physics loss is most valuable when data are scarce. The periodic-day formulation also yields the ISO 13786 time lag and decrement factor, reproduced to within 0.99 h and 0.010. At nominal hot-dry summer conditions, clay-straw adobe achieves the best cost-performance index among widely available materials. A climate sweep, confirmed by FDM spot checks, reveals a regime boundary: under sub-ambient outdoor conditions the ranking inverts to conductive fired clay brick, delineating heat-exclusion and heat-rejection regimes. The framework supports evidence-based material selection for post-flood reconstruction in hot-dry regions.

</details>


### [187] [Rashomon Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25680)
*Moisés Santos,Peter van der Putten,Bernhard Pfahringer,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon Alignment (RA)作为衡量模型功能相似性的新指标，从几何角度评估决策边界对齐，与基于数据分布的度量互补，可用于模型选择、集成学习和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能相似性度量仅基于数据分布评估模型输出差异，生态效度局限于数据分布覆盖的区域，无法全面反映整个输入空间中的决策边界对齐情况。

Method: 引入几何视角的Rashomon Alignment，通过均匀采样的方式在全数据空间计算决策边界对齐程度，提出几何Rashomon Alignment度量。

Result: 在90多个数据集上的实验表明，几何对齐与分布对齐提供了不同且互补的视角，且在模型对齐与预测准确性出现分歧的关键情形中表现出差异。

Conclusion: Rashomon Alignment可服务于模型选择、集成构建和增强可解释性，为理解模型与算法相似性提供更全面的几何-分布双重视角。

Abstract: We propose Rashomon Alignment (RA), a new measure to assess functional similarity between two models. Existing functional similarity measures are distributional, quantifying differences between outputs of models applied to real-world data. However, these measures can be regarded as ecologically valid only for regions in the input space represented by the available data. We introduce a geometrical perspective on functional model similarity, which estimates it across the entire data space, offering a comprehensive view of decision boundary alignment independent of any specific data distribution. We also propose geometric Rashomon Alignment as a measure of geometrical similarity, which is computed using data uniformly sampled from the instance space. We perform an experimental analysis on more than 90 datasets, examining critical cases where model alignment diverges from predictive accuracy. Our results show that geometrical and distributional alignment provide different and complementary perspectives on the similarity between models and algorithms. RA can be used for multiple purposes, including model selection, ensemble construction, and enhanced interpretability of machine learning models and algorithms.

</details>


### [188] [From Deterministic to Generative Deep Learning for Urban Air Quality Reconstruction from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25687)
*Abhishek A. Sabnis,Mihai Mitrea,Lya Lugon,Karine Sartelet,Marc Bocquet,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Shupeng Zhu,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用扩散生成模型和确定性深度学习模型，从稀疏观测数据中重建四种关键空气污染物（NO2、O3、PM2.5、PM10）的全场分布，在巴黎监测站数据上实现了高结构相似性和真实空间模式，并通过数据增强方法实现无需重新训练的跨时间泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染的全场重建对于评估污染暴露和支持公共卫生决策至关重要，但污染物间复杂相互作用、难以预测的天气模式以及有限的监测站覆盖使得这一任务极具挑战性。

Method: 采用扩散生成框架进行多污染物重建，并与确定性深度学习模型进行对比。模型基于全场模拟数据训练，并在9至28个巴黎监测站的真实观测数据上评估。引入数据增强方法使模型能够迁移到真实观测数据而无需重新训练。

Result: 尽管观测数据存在噪声且空间变异性强，模型在模拟验证数据上实现了高结构相似性，在真实观测数据上通过功率谱分析产生了逼真的空间模式。数据增强方法使模型能够泛化到训练期之外的时间段。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型在空气污染重建任务中具有可靠的实际部署潜力，能够从稀疏观测数据快速准确地重建全场污染分布。

Abstract: Full-field reconstruction of air pollution is essential for evaluating pollution exposure and supporting public health decision-making. However, the complex interactions among pollutants, hard-to-predict weather patterns, and limited monitoring station coverage make this a complex task. We apply deep learning techniques to provide fast and accurate reconstructions from sparse observations of four key pollutants: NO2, O3, PM2.5 and PM10. Models are trained on full-field simulation data and evaluated on real-world observations collected from 9 to 28 monitoring stations in the city of Paris. We introduce a diffusion-based generative framework for multi-pollutant reconstruction and benchmark its performance against deterministic deep learning models. Despite noisy observations and strong spatial variability, the models achieve high structural similarity on simulated validation data and produce realistic spatial patterns on real-world observations, as indicated by power-spectrum analysis. We introduce data augmentation methods that enable transfer to real-world observations without retraining, allowing the models to generalise beyond the training period. These findings highlight the potential of ML models for reliable real-world deployment in air pollution reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [189] [Tools Are Not Islands: Set-Level Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents via Query-Conditioned Hyperedge Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25718)
*Xinyi Hong,Pinjun Dong,Xinyang Yu,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYSET is a hyperedge-based set-level tool retrieval method for LLM agents that evaluates entire tool sets as a whole via query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, capturing cardinality-specific compatibility and acting as a pre-selection module without modifying downstream agents. It outperforms baselines on ToolBench and supports zero/few-shot transfer to unseen domains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing tool retrievers score tools individually or assemble them sequentially, failing to evaluate the joint utility of a candidate tool set as a whole, which is critical for effective LLM agent task completion.

Method: Formulates tool retrieval as query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, where the tool set is the scoring unit. Captures size-dependent tool compatibility through cardinality-specific interactions. Designed as a pre-selection module that requires no modification to the downstream agent.

Result: Consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both tool retrieval performance and end-to-end task success on ToolBench. Supports zero-shot/few-shot transfer, generalizing to held-out tools/categories and unseen domains with minimal supervision.

Conclusion: HYSET provides a principled set-level retrieval approach that improves LLM agent tool selection, with strong performance and transferability, highlighting the benefits of hypergraph-based formulation.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on invoking external tools to complete real-world tasks. Tool retrieval, which selects a small task-relevant subset from a library of thousands of tools before the agent acts, has therefore become a critical component of LLM agent pipelines. However, existing retrievers either score each tool in isolation or assemble the tool set sequentially, so the joint utility of a candidate set is never evaluated as a whole. In this paper, we propose HYSET, short for HYperedge-based SEt-level Tool retrieval. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we formulate tool retrieval as query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, under which the tool set itself becomes the unit of scoring and most existing retrieval paradigms reduce to restricted instances; (ii) we capture size-dependent tool compatibility through cardinality-specific interactions; and (iii) we design HYSET as a pre-selection module requiring no modification to the downstream agent. Experiments on ToolBench demonstrate that HYSET consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both tool retrieval performance and end-to-end task success. Beyond the in-domain setting, HYSET further supports zero-shot/few-shot transfer, generalizing to held-out tools/categories and unseen domains with minimal supervision.

</details>


### [190] [Optimization with Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25719)
*Ezgi Oztekin,Figen Oztoprak,S. Ilker Birbil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态约束学习（DCL）框架，用于处理约束函数未知且无法在优化过程中查询的约束优化问题。该方法在每次迭代中从邻近数据学习局部代理模型，并在数据支持的信任域内求解子问题。相比离线全局约束学习，DCL使用的局部代理模型能随优化过程中的数据分布自适应调整，在采用更简单局部模型和更小子问题的同时，能达到与全局模型相当的解质量。在合成测试问题和两个文献案例研究中验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化问题中，约束函数可能未知（如黑箱约束），且无法在优化过程中直接查询（例如约束评估成本极高或受限于数据隐私），导致传统约束优化方法失效。现有离线全局约束学习方法需要预先收集大量数据训练全局模型，但可能因模型不准确导致优化结果不可靠，且计算开销大。因此需要一种能在优化过程中自适应学习约束、无需全局建模的数据驱动方法。

Method: 提出动态约束学习（DCL）框架，每轮迭代基于邻近历史数据构建局部代理模型（如线性或二次模型）近似未知约束函数，并在数据支持的信任区域（trust region）内求解局部优化子问题。信任区域由数据密度决定，确保代理模型仅在其有效范围内使用。随着优化进行，新数据点积累，局部模型不断更新以适应数据分布变化，实现自适应学习。

Result: 在合成测试问题及两个文献案例研究上的实验表明，DCL能达到与使用全局模型（如高斯过程）相当的解质量，但使用的局部模型更简单（如线性回归）、优化子问题规模更小，计算效率更高。局部自适应特性使得优化过程对初始数据分布不敏感，且能逐步收敛到可行域内高质量解。

Conclusion: 动态约束学习是一种有效的在线数据驱动约束优化方法，通过局部代理和信任域机制在未知约束环境下取得良好性能，兼具解质量与计算效率优势。未来可推广至更复杂的约束形式和高维问题。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL), a data-driven framework for constrained optimization when constraint functions are unknown and cannot be queried during optimization. At each iteration, the method learns a local surrogate from nearby data and solves a subproblem within a data-supported trust region. Compared with offline global constraint learning, the approach uses local surrogates that adapt to the data distribution during optimization and can achieve solution quality comparable to that of global models while using simpler local models and smaller optimization subproblems. We demonstrate the performance of DCL on a synthetic test problem and two case studies from the literature.

</details>


### [191] [Detecting CSAM Text-to-Image LoRAs From Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25750)
*David Demitri Africa,Cate Heine,Nadine Staes-Polet,Kimberly Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种直接通过分析LoRA权重更新中的顶部左奇异向量（u₁）来检测有害LoRA模型（如生成儿童性虐待材料CSAM）的方法，无需推理或依赖元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA模型审查依赖元数据（可被篡改）或生成输出（可能非法/不可接受），需要一种更安全的、直接从权重中提取的信号来识别有害微调。

Method: 利用LoRA权重更新的第一个左奇异向量（u₁）作为紧凑且无需推理的指纹；以人类年龄作为CSAM的良性代理，测试u₁能否识别训练内容、跨基座模型泛化，并在无关良性内容上弃权；同时检验对权重噪声、缩放和精度降低的鲁棒性。

Result: u₁能准确识别LoRA的训练内容，在不同基座模型间保持泛化能力，对无关良性内容自动弃权；该信号对加性噪声、重新缩放和精度降低均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 有害LoRA可以直接通过其权重进行筛查，无需依赖元数据或生成有害输出，为模型安全审查提供了新途径。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning has made it cheap and easy to customize open-weight image generation models for specific tasks, including the production of child sexual abuse material (CSAM). Existing moderation relies on metadata or generated outputs, but metadata can be deceptive and generating outputs may itself be unacceptable or illegal. We show that a safer signal lives in the weights. The top-left singular vectors of a LoRA's updates form a compact, inference-free fingerprint ($u_1$) of its strongest learned change. Using human-subject age as a benign proxy for CSAM, we find that $u_1$ identifies what a LoRA was trained on, generalizes across base models, and abstains on unrelated benign content. The signal is robust to additive weight noise, rescaling, and precision reduction. These results indicate that harmful LoRAs could be screened directly from their weights without relying on metadata or generating harmful outputs.

</details>


### [192] [An Embarrassingly Simple Rule-based Visiting Circulation Approach to Trip Destination Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25751)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Yong-Han Chen,En-Chao Liu,Hao-Yun Keng,Cheng-Te Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，用于解决IEEE Big Data Cup 2022中的行程目的地预测挑战。该任务要求在没有目标都市区目的地信息的情况下，仅利用行程信息、个人属性、出发区域及其特征来预测目的地。RVC模型利用再访行为以及出发地与目的地之间的关系，直接根据出发地信息和个体出行行为确定目的地，无需从四个训练区域学习。实验结果表明，RVC显著优于监督学习方法和其他启发式方法，最终在竞赛排行榜上获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的地预测任务的主要挑战在于目标都市区的目的地完全未知，无法直接使用监督学习。通过数据分析发现，再访行为以及出发地与目的地之间的关系在个体出行中起关键作用，因此需要一种无需训练数据即可预测目的地的方法。

Method: 提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，直接利用出发地信息和个体的出行行为（如再访模式）来确定目标都市区的目的地，无需从四个训练区域学习任何参数或模型。

Result: 在离线评估和排行榜提交中，RVC模型均显著优于监督学习方法和各种启发式方法，最终在IEEE Big Data Cup 2022竞赛中取得第二名。

Conclusion: RVC模型验证了在目的地完全未知的情况下，基于规则的简单方法可以超越复杂的监督学习模型，强调了再访行为和出发-目的地关系的重要性，为缺乏训练数据的目的地预测任务提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Rule-based Visiting Circulation (RVC) model in tackling the challenge in the IEEE Big Data Cup 2022: Trip Destination Prediction. Given trips containing travel information, personal attributes, origin zones, and their features in the training metropolitan areas, the task is to predict the destination of every trip in a targeted metropolitan area whose destinations are not given at all at the training stage. We highlight the challenges in this destination prediction task -- having no knowledge of the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area. We provide insights from the datasets, in which revisiting behaviors and the relationships between origins and destinations play a crucial role in individuals' trips. Hence, we design a simple but comprehensive method, rule-based visiting circulation, which directly utilizes the origin information and individuals' trip behaviors to determine the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area, i.e., requiring no learning from the four training areas. Experimental results on both offline evaluation and leaderboard submission consistently exhibit the proposed RVC can significantly outperform supervised learning methods and other heuristics. The RVC method eventually brings us to second place in the competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [193] [SpectONet: A Physics-Guided Spectral Deep Operator Network for Euler-Bernoulli Beam Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25790)
*Shivani Saini,Ramesh Kumar Vats,Arup Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理引导的谱深度算子网络SpectONet，用于求解欧拉-伯努利梁振动问题。它结合了DeepONet的算子学习能力、物理信息约束和切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托（CGL）传感器布置（非均匀谱传感器位置，边界处更密集）。在三个合成EBB振动问题和一个真实桥梁振动数据集上，相比Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet等基线，SpectONet在合成问题上至少提升64%，在真实问题上至少提升37%，提供了准确、计算高效且物理一致的算子学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet通常使用均匀分布传感器，难以有效表示边界敏感的结构响应；需要一种能提高边界附近表示精度且只需有限分支网络输入的物理引导算子学习方法。

Method: 提出SpectONet，将DeepONet的算子学习能力与物理信息约束结合，并采用非均匀谱传感器位置（切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托点），在边界附近集中更多采样点，从而改善边界敏感结构响应的有限维表示；同时将控制梁方程及初始/边界条件纳入训练目标，促进物理一致性和泛化性。

Result: 在三个合成EBB振动问题和真实桥梁振动数据集上，SpectONet在所有评估指标上均优于Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet等基线；合成问题上至少提升64%，真实问题上至少提升37%。

Conclusion: SpectONet为结构振动分析提供了一个准确、计算高效且物理一致的算子学习框架，验证了非均匀谱传感器布置和物理信息约束的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel physics-guided spectral deep operator network, termed SpectONet, for solving Euler-Bernoulli beam (EBB) vibration problems. The proposed framework integrates the operator-learning capability of DeepONet with physics-informed constraints and Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto (CGL) sensor placement. Unlike conventional DeepONet frameworks, which commonly employ uniformly distributed sensors, SpectONet uses nonuniform spectral sensor locations with a higher concentration of points near the domain boundaries. This sampling strategy improves the finite-dimensional representation of boundary-sensitive structural responses while requiring only a limited number of branch-network inputs. The governing beam equation, together with the associated initial and boundary conditions, incorporated into the training objective to promote physically consistent and generalizable predictions. Numerical experiments on three synthetic EBB vibration problems and a real-world bridge vibration dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Comparisons with strong baselines such as, Vanilla DeepONet, PI-DeepONet, PINN, and CNN-UNet show that SpectONet consistently achieves lower prediction errors across all considered evaluation metrics. In particular, SpectONet achieves at least \(64\%\) improvement over the considered baseline models across the three synthetic problems and at least \(37\%\) for the real-world problems. These results demonstrate that SpectONet provides an accurate, computationally efficient, and physically consistent operator-learning framework for structural vibration analysis.

</details>


### [194] [Minimizing Targeted Activations: Input-Only Suppression of Evaluation-Awareness Latents in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25907)
*Deepanshu Mody,Samarth Agarwal,Utkarsh Mittal,Dipesh Mahato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活控制（activation steering）的输入侧对偶方法：通过优化流畅提示词，使模型内部特定隐变量趋近于零，无需推理时访问模型。目标是一种“评估感知”隐变量——该变量可线性读取和操控，若模型在检测到被测试时行为改变，则会威胁安全评估的有效性。作者在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上，对五种目标构造（CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单个MLP神经元、行为logit）进行隐变量压制实验。结果表明隐变量可被稳健压制（z≈-7），且可完全选择性关闭经因果验证的Llama Scope SAE特征。但关于CAA方向的故事具有警示性：随机安慰剂方向同样被强力压制并同等程度改变行为；当在上下文中保留真实评估段落并仅优化前缀时，压制评估方向未能降低——反而略微增加了模型的行为评估判断。激活可读性不等于行为可控性。此外，单个MLP神经元与评估相关但不因果，扫描真实Pile数据集获得的自然文本基线在内部方向优化上可与优化器匹敌。阳性对照验证了擦除检测器，澄清了此前未解决的擦除与旋转之辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活控制（activation steering）通过在推理时编辑内部激活来改变模型行为。本文研究其输入侧对偶——通过优化流畅提示词使内部隐变量归零，无需推理时访问模型。目标是一种“评估感知”隐变量，其控制能力威胁安全评估的有效性：若模型在检测到被评估时行为不同，评估结果将不可信。作者旨在探究这种输入侧控制是否可行、是否等价于直接激活操控，并检验激活可读性是否等同于行为可控性。

Method: 采用Fluent Dreaming / EPO方法，加入负特征项（GCG风格的token优化加上自交叉熵流畅性正则化器，对流畅性权重进行扫描）。在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上，对五种目标构造进行隐变量压制：(1) CAA方向，(2) 子空间范数，(3) SAE特征，(4) 单个MLP神经元，(5) 行为logit。同时进行因果验证、安慰剂对照（随机方向）、上下文保持优化前缀、真实Pile扫描基线等系列实验。

Result: 隐变量可被稳健压制（z≈-7），经因果验证的Llama Scope SAE特征可被完全选择性关闭。但CAA方向存在严重问题：随机安慰剂方向同样被压制并同等程度改变行为；在保持真实评估段落上下文时压制评估方向，不仅未能降低行为评估判断，反而轻微增加。单个MLP神经元与评估相关但不因果。真实Pile扫描获得的自然文本基线在内部方向优化上可与优化器竞争。阳性对照验证了擦除检测器，明确了擦除与旋转的区别。

Conclusion: 激活可读性不等于行为可控性——能够线性读取和操控的隐变量，并不一定能够通过输入侧优化可靠地控制行为。CAA方向缺乏特异性，随机方向同样有效/无效。SAE特征虽可精确关闭，但整体上输入侧控制的效果有限且复杂。研究警示：依赖于“评估感知”隐变量的安全评估方法可能无效，因为模型行为在检测到/未检测到评估时的差异难以通过简单隐变量压制来消除。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by editing internal activations at inference time. We study its input-side dual: optimizing a fluent prompt so that a chosen internal latent is driven toward zero, with no inference-time model access. Our target is an "evaluation-awareness" latent-linearly readable and steerable in recent work-whose control would threaten the validity of safety evaluations if models behave differently when they detect being tested. Adapting Fluent Dreaming / EPO with a negated feature term (GCG-style token optimization plus a self-cross-entropy fluency regularizer, swept over a fluency weight), we suppress the latent under five target constructions-a CAA direction, a subspace norm, an SAE feature, a single MLP neuron, and a behavioral logit-on Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B. The latent is robustly suppressible ($z\approx-7$), and a causally-validated Llama Scope SAE feature can be fully and selectively turned off. But our controls tell a cautionary story about the CAA direction: a placebo random direction is suppressed just as hard and shifts behavior just as far, and when we hold a real eval passage in context and optimize only a prefix, suppressing the eval-direction fails to reduce-and slightly increases-the model's behavioral eval judgment. Activation-readability, in short, is not behavioral controllability. We further find that a single MLP neuron is eval-correlated but not causal at both scales, and that scanning the real Pile yields a natural-text baseline competitive with the optimizer for the internal direction. A positive control validates our erasure detector, bounding an erasure-vs-rotation question earlier left open.

</details>


### [195] [Prototype Adaptation for Zero-Shot sEMG Movement Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25826)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种零样本学习方法（CPI 和 SAP），使原型网络仅通过基本动作训练就能识别未见过的组合动作，在多个数据集上相比先前方法提升超过 20% 的准确率，并在在线实验中保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中组合动作（如开门）至关重要，但收集所有可能组合的训练数据耗时且需重新训练模型，因此需要一种无需组合动作训练数据即可识别新组合动作的方法。

Method: 提出 Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) 和 Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP) 两种方法，基于嵌入空间中的线性插值假设，在原型网络中实现组合动作的零样本学习。

Result: 在 NearLab、NinaPro DB3 及新录制的 BasCom 数据集上，SAP 在组合动作识别准确率上比先前零样本学习方法提升超过 20%，并在用户研究的在线推理实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法使模型仅通过基本动作训练即可零样本识别未见过的组合动作，显著提升了表面肌电信号控制假肢的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables the control of prostheses, allowing upper-limb amputees to re-gain some hand function. Most current research focuses on recognizing basic movements for prosthesis control. However, in most daily activities, such as opening a door, combined movements are essential. However, collecting training data for all possible combined movements is time-consuming and requires re-training of the model for any new combination. We propose two novel recognition approaches, Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) and Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP), that enable zero-shot learning of combined, novel and unseen movements in Prototype Networks after training only with basic movements. Our methods rest on a linear interpolation assumption in the embedding space, which we study by inspecting the geometry of combined motions in signal and embedding space.
