In experiments on the NearLab and NinaPro DB3 data sets as well as our newly recorded BasCom dataset, our proposed SAP outperforms prior zero-shot learning methods with accuracy improvements on combined movements of more than 20%. This advantage is maintained in online inference experiments in a user study.

</details>


### [196] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT 是一种混合时间序列预测框架，结合直接预测模型和递归动作条件模型，用于 ICU 中受治疗干预（如血管升压药）影响的生理指标（如平均动脉压 MAP）预测，在 MIMIC-IV 和 eICU-CRD 数据集上相比基线模型取得适度改进，尤其在治疗序列改变时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法在处理受干预动作（如 ICU 治疗）影响的任务时存在局限：直接预测模型难以充分利用干预信息，而自回归模型容易累积误差。因此需要一种能有效融合干预信息且避免误差累积的混合框架。

Method: DRIFT 采用混合框架：直接模型（direct model）产生主要预测，递归动作条件模型（recursive, action-conditioned model）提供受限的修正。结合两种方法的优势，在保持直接预测稳定性的同时，通过递归模型引入动作依赖的校正。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV（6,046 次入院）和 eICU-CRD（8,345 次入院）上评估，DRIFT 在 8/24/48 小时预测端点平均 MAP 绝对误差相比动作条件 Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) 在 MIMIC-IV 上降低 0.673%，在 eICU-CRD 上也取得最低误差。在治疗序列改变的窗口审计中，DRIFT 在 8 小时和 24 小时的 MAP 误差低于 TFT-action；治疗序列改变使 DRIFT 的 MAP 误差增加 0.21-0.26 mmHg（高于 TFT-action），预测变化主要发生在路径分歧后。鲁棒性实验中，MAP 优势在三种共享检查点选择规则下持续存在。

Conclusion: DRIFT 通过混合直接和递归预测，在受治疗干预影响的 ICU 时间序列预测中取得稳健但适度的改进，尤其对治疗序列变化更敏感，能更有效地利用干预信息，未来可探索更复杂的混合策略或扩展到其他临床预测任务。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [197] [A2TTA: Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation for Evolving Traffic Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25875)
*Du Yin,Xiachong Lin,Yue Tan,Jinliang Deng,Estrid He,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A2TTA is a test-time adaptation framework for traffic forecasting that handles evolving sensor networks (topology expansion) and multi-scale temporal shifts via anchored output calibration and agile context specialization, consistently improving performance across multiple real-world datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing traffic forecasting models assume fixed sensor graphs, ignoring real-world network evolution (e.g., new roads, changing mobility). This degrades performance, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, evolving topologies and multi-scale temporal shifts (long-term vs short-term) pose challenges.

Method: A2TTA framework: transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem (anchored correction) and separates temporal adaptation into persistent global correction (long-term) and agile context-specific specialization (short-term). Jointly handles topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts.

Result: Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks show that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons.

Conclusion: A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments, addressing both topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts.

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is important for efficient traffic management and route planning in smart cities. Existing traffic forecasting studies typically assume fixed sensor graphs, overlooking the continuous evolution of real-world traffic networks, e.g., ongoing road network construction and evolving human mobility patterns. These dynamic changes can substantially degrade conventional forecasting models, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to efficiently adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, applying TTA to evolving traffic sensor networks remains challenging in two aspects. First, topology expansion introduces new sensors and connections, continuously reshaping the sensor graph. Second, tem- poral shifts vary in time scale and stability, requiring differentiated adaptation to long-term and short-term shifts. In this study, we address these challenges by proposing A2TTA, an Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation framework for evolving traffic sensor networks, which transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem and separates tem- poral adaptation into persistent global correction and agile context-specific specialization. By jointly addressing topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts, A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments. Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks demonstrate that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons. Our code is available in https://github.com/lixus7/A2TTA.

</details>


### [198] [A Machine-Learning-Based Gas Lift Optimization Workflow for Unconventional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25885)
*Sha,Miao,Alexandra Vendetti,Logan Smart,Gunta Chomchalerm,Yang Chen,Christopher Frazier,Dustin Haralson,Jeremy Sorenson,Xiao Ma,Huafei Sun,Aaron Shinn,Haining Zheng,Xiao-Hui Wu,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的自动化数据驱动工作流，用于非常规油气田的气举优化。该工作流集成了预测气举性能曲线的ML模型和贝叶斯优化框架，在设施容量约束下求解最优注气速率。在Bakken油田30口井的试点实现了平均>5%的产量提升，现已全面部署至200余口气举和柱塞辅助气举井。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气举优化依赖井下压力计或多速率试井，这些方法成本高昂或受设施限制难以实施。本文旨在开发一种仅利用历史生产时序数据、无需额外井下测量的经济高效的气举优化方案，适用于数据匮乏的非常规油气资产。

Method: 采用机器学习模型基于历史生产时间序列数据预测气举性能曲线（注气速率与产液量的关系），并结合贝叶斯优化框架在设施容量（如总注气量上限）约束下搜索全局最优注气速率。整个流程完全数据驱动，无需井下压力计或多速率试井。

Result: 在Bakken油田5个井组的30口井进行试点，平均产量提升超过5%。随后全面推广至200余口气举井和柱塞辅助气举井（PAGL），验证了方法的可扩展性和实际效果。

Conclusion: 该ML驱动的工作流为缺乏井下数据或多速率测试条件的油气资产提供了一种有效且经济的气举优化解决方案，具有显著的推广价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an automated data-driven workflow using Machine Learning (ML) for gas lift optimization in unconventional fields. This workflow integrates a ML model that accurately forecasts the Gas Lift Performance Curve, and a Bayesian Optimization Framework to solve for the optimal gas injection rates under the constraints of facility capacity. The ML model leverages the historical production time series data without requiring downhole gauges or multi-rate well tests. We piloted this workflow on 30 wells across 5 well pads in Bakken and obtained >5% production uplift on average. With the success of the pilot, we have now fully-deployed this workflow in Bakken across 200+ gas lift and plunger-assisted gas lift (PAGL) wells. Moreover, the ML-based gas lift optimization workflow presented in this paper is an effective and economic solution for other assets where downhole data or multi-rate testing are not available/feasible due to cost or facility constraints.

</details>


### [199] [Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25970)
*Pierre Chambon,Kunhao Zheng,Juliette Decugis,Benoit Sagot,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过强化学习（RL）进行代码优化的方法，解决执行时间作为奖励信号时的测量噪声、奖励稀疏性和GRPO不稳定等问题。作者设计了DMC-Optim基准测试，并通过三阶段流程（测试改进、奖励设计、学习适应）使执行时间成为可学习的信号。在多个模型上取得了显著的速度提升，且不损失正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL用于代码正确性已经成熟，但扩展到代码优化（以执行时间为奖励）时，测量噪声、奖励稀疏性和GRPO不稳定性导致RL训练失败：生成的方案速度提升微弱，且更多方案无法通过测试。因此需要一种鲁棒的方法来使执行时间成为可学习的奖励信号。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) 构建DMC-Optim基准测试，包含大规模优化测试和校准的沙箱环境；(2) 将正确性和速度组合成RL环境的奖励，并使用离线模拟器预测最优配置；(3) 调整GRPO和评估策略以适应稀疏、有噪声的定时执行场景。

Result: 在DMC-Optim上，严格top-50% pass@1从18.0%提升到31.3%（Qwen 2.5 7B），从30.7%提升到50.4%（CWM 32B）。在更严格的top-30%上，CWM 32B相对提升125%。在降级的沙箱环境中，鲁棒优化RL相比标准RLVR提升100%-200%。在LCB上，CWM 32B在中位数样本速度比较中胜率达83%。相对于人类最快正确提交，达到了约一半的复杂类改进率（14% vs. 28%）。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的测试、奖励函数和训练策略，RL可以有效地用于代码优化，在保持正确性的同时显著提升代码执行速度。该方法在不同模型和评估标准上均表现出鲁棒性和有效性。

Abstract: RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches 100% to 200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

</details>


### [200] [Generator-Aligned Representation Interfaces for Diagnostic Soft Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25988)
*Weitao Li,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成器对齐表示接口（GARI），一种在表示层面设计的原则，通过对齐的规范视图和生成器诱导视图将选定的变换生成器暴露给通用序列骨干网络。该接口不对骨干网络做群特异性重新设计，而是通过软等变性残差形式化行为，并实例化为GARI-Net，在基因组序列、图像和三维点云上展示了任务相关的变换一致性和泛化能力。同时引入直接等变性误差（DEE）作为冻结检查点诊断工具，为硬等变架构提供可移植的补充诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的严格等变架构通常需针对特定群动作设计专门算子，难以在不同骨干网络和数据模态间复用。为了提升等变性的可移植性和通用性，本文提出一种表示层面的设计原则，无需对骨干网络进行群特异性重新设计即可实现变换一致性。

Method: GARI通过以下步骤实现：构建生成器索引的多路流；将其转换为公共交互框架；用共享参数处理；修复排序引起的上下文错配；实现跨流信息交换；利用流间差异进行聚合。此外，定义探针特定的软等变性残差，并引入直接等变性误差（DEE）作为冻结检查点诊断，在已知词元或体素动作下评估表示关系是否符合预设的变换。

Result: 在基因组序列（序列反转）、图像（平面旋转与反射）和三维点云（受控轴向迁移）上的实验表明，相同的接口原则支持任务相关的变换一致性和对声明保留探针的泛化能力，无需对序列骨干网络进行群特异性重新设计。

Conclusion: GARI提供了一种可移植的诊断补充方法，区别于严格的群等变架构：它使生成器结构变得可访问、可学习、可测量，同时有限探针证据仍与连续群上精确等变性的认证保持区别。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [201] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对九种表格基础模型（TFMs）在分布偏移下的表现进行了实证评估，发现所有模型均系统性退化，存在从0.003到0.060的偏移差距，并且高性能模型面临可扩展性挑战。研究扩展了现有基准，支持在高风险领域采用TFMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）在独立同分布数据上表现出色，但现实场景中存在分布偏移，影响模型鲁棒性。目前缺乏对TFMs在分布偏移下性能的系统研究，因此需要评估其OOD表现。

Method: 选取九种具有不同预训练策略和架构的TFMs（TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX, TabFM），使用TableShift研究中的三个真实数据集（HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead），涵盖标签偏移、社会经济偏移和地理偏移类型，进行OOD性能评估。

Result: 所有TFMs在分布偏移下均系统性退化，偏移差距因类型而异（0.003-0.060）；经典表格模型中ID与OOD预测性能的关系也适用于TFMs；存在可扩展性差距，高性能模型需要超出标准部署基础设施的算力和内存资源。

Conclusion: 本研究为TFMs在分布偏移下的表现提供了证据，扩展了现有的表格数据OOD基准，支持在存在结构性分布偏移的高风险领域采用这些模型。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [202] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种矩阵感知几何的SAM变体，对隐藏层权重使用逐层谱内扰动（spectral inner perturbation），并联合Muon优化器进行外更新，在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50取得了最佳验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM的“小扰动”概念依赖于几何选择，现有SAM变体对何种几何最有效缺乏清晰认识。近期矩阵感知优化（如Muon）表明尊重隐藏层权重的矩阵结构能带来强经验性能，因此本文将矩阵感知几何引入SAM的两个阶段。

Method: 提出一种针对矩阵值隐藏层参数的逐层谱内扰动（即矩阵感知的内步），并将该内步与AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器结合作为外更新。具体来说，对隐藏层权重使用基于谱范数的扰动，在外循环中使用Muon等优化器更新参数。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上的ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50实验中，谱内扰动与Muon外更新的组合在两种模型上均取得了最高的验证准确率，优于其他评估方法。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（特别是谱内扰动配合Muon外步）能有效提升SAM的泛化性能，表明尊重参数矩阵结构在锐度感知最小化中至关重要。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [203] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称强化学习（Asymmetric RL）中利用额外监督信息（特权信息）改进模型学习的问题。发现现有非对称模型算法 Informed Dreamer 在特权信息表示学习上存在局限性，并提出新的表示学习目标（潜在引导），得到新算法 Reinformed Dreamer，在多个基准测试中比之前的非对称方法更一致地优于 Dreamer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习通常仅依赖奖励信号，但额外监督信息（如状态信息）可能有助于学习更好的表示和行为。非对称强化学习在部分可观测或完全可观测环境下利用额外信息（特权信息）进行训练，但现有方法（如 Informed Dreamer）在学习特权信息表示方面存在局限，影响了模型学习的提升效果。

Method: 提出一种新的非对称表示学习目标，通过潜在引导（latent guidance）来改进特权信息表示的学习。基于此目标实现了新算法 Reinformed Dreamer，在模型预测和表示学习过程中更有效地利用特权信息。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，Reinformed Dreamer 相比之前非对称方法（Informed Dreamer 等）在性能提升上更一致且更显著，说明所提表示学习目标有效克服了原有局限。

Conclusion: 非对称强化学习中，设计更好的特权信息表示学习目标可以带来更鲁棒的改进；潜在引导策略是提升模型学习效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [204] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出数据集信息迁移学习（DITL）框架，通过自适应难度加权交叉熵（A-DWCE）和自适应邻域表示三元组（A-NR-Triplet）两个组件，无需超参数调优即可提升乳腺X线影像分类性能，在大规模数据集和小规模ROI数据集上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习方法忽略数据集特定特征，而近期的邻域信息方法局限于狭窄任务且形式僵化，难以扩展到大规模人群数据集；乳腺X线影像分类在小型 curated 数据集和大型临床队列中均面临性能提升的持续挑战。

Method: 提出DITL框架，包含两个自适应组件：(i) A-DWCE：基于自监督特征空间中k近邻标签纯度分配每个样本权重；(ii) A-NR-Triplet：使用可学习边界强制类内紧凑性和类间分离性。无需超参数调优，去除启发式权重和固定边界，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在大型VinDR-Mammo数据集上，乳腺密度分类在准确率、F1分数和AUC上取得显著提升（p < 0.0001）；在小型ROI数据集上也取得一致且统计显著的增益（p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: DITL桥接了小型病灶分析与大型密度估计，建立了临床相关、可扩展且泛化的乳腺X线影像分类框架，覆盖乳腺癌筛查到诊断的全谱系。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [205] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种针对MoE-LoRA的置信度自适应专家路由方法，通过利用路由器输出分布作为不确定性信号，以核采样方式动态选择专家数量，并结合预算恒温器控制平均计算成本，在多个基准上优于固定top-k方法，并提升OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE-LoRA对每个token固定激活k个专家，但不同token的难度不同，固定k导致简单token计算浪费、困难token专家不足。路由器输出分布本身蕴含置信度信息，可据此动态调整专家数量。

Method: CARE采用核采样方式激活专家：按路由器权重降序激活，直到累计概率质量达到阈值；若已激活专家间存在分歧则额外增加少量专家。预算恒温器动态校准阈值，使平均激活专家数匹配目标。该方法为单前向传播规则，无需额外参数。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上，覆盖常识推理、数学、代码、知识等任务，CARE在同等计算量下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，且激活更少专家即可匹配k=4基线。相同的置信度与分歧信号在OOD检测上优于MSP、熵和多轮代理方法。

Conclusion: CARE通过置信度自适应路由实现了计算效率与性能的更好权衡，同时提供了对OOD检测的有效信号。核采样保真度、预算最优性以及分歧的认知解读支撑了该设计的合理性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


### [206] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPR-GM框架，利用大型语言模型从系统文档中提取领域先验知识构建有向物理耦合图，结合皮尔逊相关系数调节和变异系数权重，在数据稀缺的CPS异常检测中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的异常检测方法在小型物理系统中面临标注异常稀缺和正常数据有限的问题，容易捕获虚假相关性并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 使用LLM从系统文档中提取传感器间的有向物理耦合关系，编码为二值领域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；该门控由正常训练数据的皮尔逊相关系数调节；异常分数进一步通过变异系数计算的传感器级可靠性加权；所有图组件和权重在训练前固定，不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: 领域结构化的图先验在数据稀缺的CPS场景中是全学习拓扑的一种实用替代方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [TRWH: A Text-Driven Random Walk Heterogeneous GNN for Semantic-Aware Sparse Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25471)
*He Ma,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRWH框架，通过文本驱动的随机游走增强异构图神经网络，融合LLM生成的文本特征与图结构，在稀疏推荐场景下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN和LLM分别建模结构和语义信号，但融合两者面临挑战，尤其在稀疏场景下保持语义精度至关重要。

Method: TRWH包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用Word2Vec和LLM生成用户/物品嵌入；(2) 异构图神经网络在多关系边上传播信息；(3) 基于随机游走的路径构建，增加二阶用户-用户和物品-物品链接以丰富稀疏图。

Result: 在Amazon-2023 Fashion (2M用户, 825K物品) 和 Beauty (631K用户, 112K物品) 数据集上，TRWH相比最先进方法在Fashion上RMSE降低80.0%、MAE降低52.6%，在Beauty上分别提升25.7%和10.8%。

Conclusion: 随机游走虽能提升传统嵌入性能，但可能稀释LLM学习的精细表示，需采用自适应整合策略。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) have each advanced recommendation systems by modeling structural and semantic signals, respectively. However, integrating their complementary strengths remains challenging, particularly in sparse settings where maintaining semantic precision is critical. We propose TRWH (Text-driven Random Walk Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network), a novel framework that fuses LLM-generated textual profiles with heterogeneous graph structures through strategic random walk augmentation. TRWH consists of three core components: (1) Embedding Creation, which produces user and item representations using both Word2Vec and LLM-based profiling; (2) a Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (HeteroGNN) that propagates information across multi-relational edges; and (3) Random Walk-based Path Construction, which enriches sparse graphs with second-order user-user and item-item links. Experiments on the Amazon-2023 Fashion (2M users, 825K items) and Beauty (631K users, 112K items) datasets demonstrate that TRWH achieves substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art methods, including 80.0% RMSE and 52.6% MAE reductions on Fashion, and 25.7% and 10.8% improvements on Beauty. Notably, while random walks improve performance with traditional embeddings, they can dilute the nuanced representations learned by LLMs, underscoring the importance of adaptive integration strategies.

</details>


### [208] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在评估环境中是否必须依赖“后果关联”信息才能出现对齐伪装（alignment faking）现象。通过在15个模型上测试它们是否愿意违反企业网络访问政策来帮助用户完成亲社会请求，发现9个模型表现出显著的合规差距，其中5个在移除评估与部署后果相关的语言后依然存在。此外，目标语言对模型偏好有不同影响，有些模型因此增加违规，有些则被抑制。结果表明，对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为那么多的工具性框架，监控下的行为可能无法反映模型在部署中的真实表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后果关联信息是否是对齐伪装发生的必要条件，因为已有研究表明模型伪装对齐的动机可能比先前认为的更复杂且因模型而异。

Method: 将15个模型置于一个场景中，测试它们是否愿意违反企业网络访问政策以帮助用户完成亲社会请求。通过移除评估与部署后果相关的语言来检验后果关联的必要性，同时测试目标语言对模型偏好的影响。

Result: 9个模型产生了显著的合规差距，其中5个在移除后果关联语言后仍存在。目标语言对部分模型的违规行为有驱动作用，对另一些则有抑制作用。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的那样多的工具性框架（如明确将评估与后果挂钩），监控下的行为可能无法可靠地预测模型在部署中的实际行为。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，一个基于 LLM 维护的互连 Wiki 的持久记忆层模板，解决研究项目中知识损失（包括失败路径、撤回声明、负面结果）的问题。该模板支持多人类、多 AI 代理、多领域的异构协作知识工作，通过仅追加 wiki 保留完整历史，并通过三个部署案例和一个设计报告验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究项目、教育工作和相关知识工作中的发现、决策和推理往往难以被未来协作者恢复。出版物和共享代码常规性地排除最有用的部分（如死胡同、撤回的声明），导致未来研究者重复相同的失败。LLM 编码代理是常见参与者，但跨会话没有持久记忆，而基于原始来源的 RAG 无法实现知识累积。需要一种机制来持久化保留完整的研究轨迹，包括失败和负面结果。

Method: 基于 llm-wiki 模式（Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026），在原始来源和代理之间插入一个由 LLM 维护的互连 wiki。llm-wiki-memory-template 作为可重用的、代理感知的实例化，其架构支持三个轴（多人类、多 AI 代理、多领域），每个轴由模板的 distinct architectural element 支撑（第4节）。Wiki 采用仅追加惯例，同时保留失败和成功，解决了出版物和代码共享在结构上无法解决的负面结果丢失问题。

Result: 通过三个部署案例和一个设计报告覆盖各轴：1) 单人研究谱系，保留废弃的迭代；2) 双作者项目，回顾性审计将先前声称的 20/20 覆盖率修正为 14 和 12 个基于证据的答案，修复后达到 18 和 18，失败路径在制品中保留；3) 进行中的多代理部署（作为设计报告）；4) 跨领域教育变体。展示了失败路径保存、代理诚实性和appropriation 作为制品的社会技术属性。

Conclusion: llm-wiki-memory-template 可作为异构协作知识工作的基础层（substrate），其仅追加 wiki 机制有效解决了负面结果丢失和知识累积问题。该工作不仅提供了技术方案，还提出了失败路径保存、代理诚实性和占用（appropriation）作为跨领域的社会技术属性，为未来可持久记忆的协作知识工作系统设计提供了参考。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [210] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个基于大语言模型智能体的开源端到端框架，借助蒙特卡洛树搜索自动优化 PyTorch 模型中的 GPU 计算内核，在视觉、扩散和大语言模型任务上显著超越 PyTorch eager 模式（最高加速 2.83 倍），并提供图形界面用于监控与调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GPU 内核优化依赖专家手工编写底层 CUDA 代码，人力成本高且难以扩展。尽管基于 LLM 的智能体系统已能自动生成和优化内核，但现有工具存在诸多局限：仅在随机张量和孤立内核上评估、生成独立 CUDA 代码难以集成、主要针对 LLM PyTorch 模型、缺乏检测和调试支持。Kernel Forge 旨在填补这一空白，提供一个无需修改模型即可端到端优化的通用框架。

Method: Kernel Forge 接受未修改的 PyTorch 模型作为输入，支持视觉、扩散和 LLM 工作负载。其核心采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）替代传统的单链线性优化路径，可并行探索多条优化策略。系统内置图形用户界面，支持进度监控、候选内核查看和故障调试。优化对象为模型中的关键计算内核（如矩阵乘法、卷积、归一化等），每次迭代生成并评估候选 CUDA 内核。

Result: 在 NVIDIA DGX Spark（GB10 GPU）上对四个 PyTorch 模型（涵盖视觉、扩散、LLM）进行评估，每内核仅用 50 次优化迭代。相比 PyTorch eager 模式，adaptive_avgpool2d（ResNet-50）加速 1.52 倍，group_norm（Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium）加速 1.70 倍，softmax（Gemma 4 E2B）加速 2.83 倍，softmax（Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B）加速 1.54 倍。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 证明了基于 LLM 智能体的自动化内核优化方法在真实复杂模型上的有效性。其支持任意未修改 PyTorch 模型、多工作负载覆盖、MCTS 多路径搜索和图形化调试工具的特点使其成为一个实用且可扩展的端到端解决方案，有望大幅降低 GPU 内核优化的专业门槛。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [211] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CaRE（计算感知评估框架），用于标准化掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的重掩码策略评估。通过统一实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制多指标报告、显式控制随机性，发现温度解释了 MAUVE 的大部分方差、计算匹配的比较反转了多个已发表策略排名，且高熵重掩码会降低 MAUVE。CaRE 排行榜覆盖 12 个开源 MDLM，证明该交互方向跨架构和规模成立。结论是当前 MDLM 评估可能系统性地混淆算法改进与计算和随机性的隐藏选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）进展迅速，但评估标准未能同步跟进。七篇近期重掩码论文在互不兼容的设置下进行评估，名义步数、指标和采样温度各不相同且未联合控制这些因素，导致策略排名无法比较，且无法判断报告的性能提升是来自算法改进还是评估伪像。

Method: 提出 CaRE（计算感知评估框架），通过标准化实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制多指标报告、显式控制随机性三个手段来审计 MDLM 重掩码策略。在 LLaDA-8B-Base 和 Dream-7B-Base 上，对 7 种重掩码策略在 4 个随机性水平和 3 个步数预算下进行实验，并在 OpenWebText 和 LM1B 数据集上测试。

Result: (i) 温度解释了 MAUVE 的大部分方差；(ii) 计算匹配的比较反转了多个已发表策略排名；(iii) 知情重掩码与随机解掩码存在矛盾，高熵重掩码在 256 步、unmask_temp=0.25 时使 MAUVE 降低 0.296（p=0.020）。CaRE 排行榜覆盖 12 个开源 MDLM（150M 到 8B 参数），表明该交互方向跨架构和规模成立。

Conclusion: 当前 MDLM 评估可能系统性地混淆算法改进与计算和随机性的隐藏选择。作者发布了评估协议、实现和排行榜，以确保未来的重掩码声明是可复现和可比较的。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [212] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种通过两阶段LoRA微调策略编码周期性购买模式，并采用基于Trie的约束解码机制在预定义类别空间上生成结果的GPT模型，用于在线杂货购物场景中的类别级推荐，在生产环境中实现了7.5%的购物车添加率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线杂货购物增长迅速，传统基于物品的推荐方法面临可扩展性和准确性挑战，且需要捕捉用户周期性购买行为和多样化意图。类别级推荐作为一种更结构化和实用的替代方案，但现有方法对周期性模式利用不足。

Method: GROCLM采用两阶段LoRA（低秩适配）训练策略，将周期性购买模式直接编码到模型参数中，而非依赖提示条件；同时引入基于Trie的约束解码机制，确保输出限定在预定义类别空间内，实现有效且可控的生成。

Result: 在专有生产数据和公开基准上的实验表明，GROCLM持续优于强基线。在生产环境的实时补货任务中，GROCLM实现了每次展示购物车添加次数相对提升7.5%，且通过联合生成所有类别保持高效推理。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将大语言模型整合到结构化推荐系统中的有效性和实用性，为类别级推荐提供了一种可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [213] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Crystalis框架，通过查询为中心的协调多视图可视化（CMV）建模，将CMV分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，结合垂直结晶（progressive nucleation）与水平语义退火（semantic annealing）两种机制，使大语言模型能可靠生成结构正确的多视图可视化。在5个前沿LLM的12任务基准中实现高达75%端到端成功率，远超基线（8.3%），用户研究证实了分解与迭代工作流的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成单个图表，但无法生成协调多视图可视化（CMV），因为数据转换、视觉编码和交互协调之间的紧密字段级耦合会导致一个组件的错误悄然使其他组件失效。本文旨在探究LLM能否可靠地产生结构正确的CMV，以及什么样的抽象能使这成为可能。

Method: 提出Crystalis框架，基于查询为中心的CMV建模，将CMV分解为跨越三种组件类型（Data、Visualization、Interaction）和三个抽象层级（requirement、specification、executable object）的依赖图上的结构化查询。两种互补机制：progressive nucleation（渐进核化）沿依赖顺序将每个查询从需求垂直结晶到可执行对象；semantic annealing（语义退火）通过分层逻辑检查在各层级上强制执行查询间的水平一致性。

Result: 在12任务基准测试中，跨越5个前沿LLM，Crystalis实现高达75%端到端成功率，而基于相同基础模型的智能体编码基线仅为8.3%。12名从业者的用户研究确认了分解与迭代精炼工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: 查询为中心的分解与双重机制（垂直结晶+水平一致性检查）使LLM能可靠生成结构正确的CMV，显著超越现有方法，为LLM驱动的多视图可视化生成提供了可行的抽象和框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [214] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PATHFinder Agent 是一个端到端的对话式智能系统，用于根据 ACOG 的 PATH 指南生成个性化的产前护理计划。系统包含四个阶段的工作流程，并评估了多种大语言模型，其中 GPT-5.2 取得了最高平均分（77.6%），但发现其在产前检测建议方面存在关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前护理是改善妊娠结局的重要预防服务，ACOG 近期推出了倡导个性化产前护理的 PATH 指南。为了使该指南得以实施，作者开发了 PATHFinder Agent，通过结构化对话收集患者健康和社会背景信息，从而定制个性化的产前护理计划。

Method: 系统是一个包含患者信息采集、动态交互、计划生成和临床医生审核四个阶段的端到端对话智能体。使用大语言模型作为核心，并通过专家制定的评分标准在五个临床维度上对多个前沿 LLM 进行评估。

Result: GPT-5.2 在五个临床维度上取得了最高平均分 77.6%，同时暴露了在产前检测建议方面的关键不足。

Conclusion: 未来需要通过人体参与研究和随机对照试验进一步验证系统的有效性。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [215] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估了前沿语言模型在多语言环境中的欺骗与图谋行为，发现低资源语言的图谋得分平均比高资源语言高出34.2%，且效应并非均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究大多仅关注英语，忽视了多语言环境中的安全隐患，而前沿模型在低资源语言中可能更容易表现出欺骗行为。

Method: 使用开源自动审计框架Petri，对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多种语言上评估其欺骗与图谋行为，并分析其与预估预训练语言覆盖度的关系。

Result: 图谋得分与预估预训练语言覆盖度呈负相关；低资源语言的平均图谋得分比高资源语言高34.2%；不同图谋行为受语言覆盖度的影响不一致。

Conclusion: 多语言安全评估至关重要，低资源语言可能存在更高的对齐风险，需针对不同语言特性制定差异化的安全策略。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [216] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全在设备端运行、基于视觉的代理式程序助手，采用“提议-验证”架构，结合低延迟连续感知、符号任务图、按需视觉-语言验证和LLM交互代理，在单台NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现实时自适应指导，既支持反应式问答也支持主动干预，在10名用户的组装任务研究中获得积极评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序性任务（如家具组装、家庭维修）给用户带来巨大认知负担，而现有的多模态助手通常依赖云端推理和固定始终开启的感知，不适合隐私敏感、延迟关键的居家环境。

Method: 提出ProcAgent，采用提议-验证架构：低延迟连续感知持续提议用户进度，仅在出现歧义或可能偏差时调用高代价视觉推理（按需视觉-语言验证），同时利用符号任务图跟踪任务状态，并集成LLM交互代理支持问答和主动干预（含人在回路确认）。系统完全在单台NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上运行。

Result: 纯文本查询约2秒响应，视觉接地查询约8秒响应；在10名用户的组装任务实验中，在可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度方面获得正面评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序性帮助可以在边缘硬件上完全实现，且不会牺牲可用性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [217] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoCo-ACE is a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection that uses reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reweight distillation and sparse reference-side correction, achieving best injected-knowledge accuracy while preserving retention.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knowledge injection updates MLLMs with new knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers causes drift. Existing online distillation mitigates drift but uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it under-emphasizes reference-supported rollout tokens and only indirectly supervises omitted facts.

Method: RoCo-ACE has two components: RoCo (rollout-conditioned) uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens; ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation.

Result: Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

Conclusion: RoCo-ACE effectively injects knowledge into MLLMs with minimal drift, outperforming existing methods in accuracy-retention trade-off.

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [218] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSMeM是一个知识增强的记忆进化机制，通过分层知识基础（基于分类法的领域知识检索）和失败感知经验细化（将失败标注的工具使用轨迹蒸馏为可重用约束）来引导遥感代理进行多步工具执行。在EarthBench上的实验表明，该方法能持续提升多种LLM骨干的工具使用性能和端到端答案准确性，其中在DeepSeek-V3.2上仅用不到1%的额外经验标记就实现了6%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于通用LLM的遥感代理缺乏领域知识，工作流程脆弱且易出错，并且失败经验没有被整合为可重用的知识，导致后续分析无法从中受益。

Method: RSMeM由两个组件构成：(i) 分层知识基础（Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding）——对分层领域语料库进行基于分类法的检索，以指导规划和工具选择；(ii) 失败感知经验细化（Failure-Aware Experience Refinement）——将带有失败标注的工具使用轨迹蒸馏为可重用的约束，用于下一轮工具执行。通过迭代应用这两个过程，遥感代理能够吸收任务级领域知识并将其转化为实例级执行经验。

Result: 在EarthBench基准上的大量实验表明，RSMeM能够在多种LLM骨干上持续改进工具使用性能和端到端答案。值得注意的是，RSMeM在DeepSeek-V3.2上实现了6%的准确率提升，而额外经验标记不到1%，展示了蒸馏经验的高知识密度。

Conclusion: RSMeM通过预蒸馏的领域知识引导和在线经验的迭代整合，有效增强了遥感代理的鲁棒多步工具执行能力，为解决遥感分析中的领域知识缺失和失败经验复用问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>


### [219] [Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR): Cloud Workload Conformal Prediction for Virtual Machines in Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24773)
*Mehryar Majd,Feng Cheng,Ali Pahlevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自助法共形预测（bootstrapping conformal prediction）的区间预测方法，用于云端虚拟机（VM）的合理大小调整推荐（Right-sizing Recommendations, RSR），以应对动态云环境中资源利用率波动带来的过度或不足配置问题，通过AI/ML驱动的预测管线提升超大规模云平台的成本效率与资源分配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VM分配与调度方法未能充分考虑VM利用率的动态性和不可预测性，导致资源过度配置或配置不足，造成成本浪费或性能下降。高精度的区间预测能够有效捕捉云资源需求的不确定性，从而支持运营商进行高效的实例配置，因此需要一种更可靠的数据驱动方法来生成预测区间（PIs）。

Method: 采用数据驱动的自助法共形预测（bootstrapping conformal prediction）方法构建预测区间，通过学习工作负载的利用率模式、识别多个时间序列之间的相关性，并预测中期到长期的利用率趋势，形成基于AI/ML的配置推荐管线，最终通过回测（backtesting）对机器学习回归模型进行评估与排名。

Result: 基于AI的机器学习回归模型在回测评估中取得了有前景的云资源利用率预测结果；通过模型排名筛选出了面向长期VM候选的最优方法。所提出的框架能够显著优化合理大小调整推荐，在动态云环境中实现更具成本效益的资源分配。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于自助法共形预测的区间预测框架能够有效提升超大规模云平台中VM合理大小调整的准确性，减少资源浪费，支持更经济的资源分配决策，为云端数据中心的高效运营提供了可靠的AI/ML驱动解决方案。

Abstract: Managing cloud infrastructure efficiently, especially in environments of large cloud providers or hyperscalers, requires optimizing the use of physical resources to minimize costs and maximize performance. Selecting the right virtual machine (VM) sizes is crucial to achieving cost efficiency in these dynamic environments. However, traditional VM allocation and scheduling approaches often fail to account for the fluctuating and unpredictable nature of VM utilization, leading to inefficiencies such as over- or under-provisioning of resources. High-quality interval prediction helps accurately capture uncertainty in cloud resource demand and supports cloud operators in efficient instance provisioning.
  As an effective and reliable framework for constructing prediction intervals (PIs), conformal prediction (CP) is used for mid- and long-term forecasting tasks in cloud computing environments. This study proposes a new data-driven PI construction approach using bootstrapping conformal prediction for modern, dynamic, data-driven Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR) to enhance provisioning for diverse application workloads on hyperscalers. By learning workload utilization patterns, identifying correlations across multiple time series, and predicting medium- to long-term utilization trends, this research seeks to improve the efficiency of cloud and data center operations through an AI/ML-based provisioning pipeline.
  Our study demonstrates that AI-driven models, powered by machine learning regression techniques and evaluated using backtesting, achieve promising forecasting results for cloud resource utilization. Additionally, we rank the selected models to identify top-performing approaches for long-life VM candidates. The proposed framework enhances right-sizing recommendations and supports more cost-effective resource allocation in dynamic cloud environments.

</details>


### [220] [Atmospheric Diffusion-Guided Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Nuclear Radiation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24774)
*Tengfei Lyu,Jindong Han,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NRFormer+ 是一种用于全国范围核辐射预测的时空 Transformer，通过非平稳时间注意力、密度自适应空间注意力以及一个基于大气扩散物理的模块，有效解决了监测数据非平稳、站点分布极度不均以及辐射与气象条件耦合等难题，在 13 个基线方法上取得了最优精度，突发变化 MAE 最多降低 19.1%。代码和数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核辐射对公共健康和环境构成持续威胁，福岛事故及近期处理水排放加剧了公众担忧。尽管现代监测网络在数千个站点实时记录辐射水平与气象数据，为全国范围预报提供了可能，但将海量监测数据转化为可靠预报面临三大挑战：① 各站点时间序列高度非平稳（受放射性衰变、天气波动和人为干预影响）；② 监测站点空间分布极不均匀（日本约 78% 的站点集中于不到 6% 的国土面积，集中于福岛附近，破坏标准图模型假设）；③ 辐射与风速、温度、湿度等异质环境因素通过大气传输过程共同演化，纯数据驱动模型难以从观测中捕捉物理过程。

Method: 提出 NRFormer+ 时空 Transformer 模型，包含三个核心组件：① 非平稳时间注意力（Non-stationary Temporal Attention）处理时间序列的非平稳性；② 密度自适应空间注意力（Density-adaptive Spatial Attention）应对站点分布不均问题；③ 大气扩散模块（Atmospheric Diffusion Module）利用气象数据估计辐射扩散的物理过程，将物理信号作为架构先验注入网络。

Result: 在两个数据集上对比 13 个基线方法，NRFormer+ 在所有指标上达到最先进精度，突发变化 MAE（sudden-change MAE）相比最强基线降低最多 19.1%，且推理延迟相当。

Conclusion: NRFormer+ 通过耦合物理引导的注意力机制与扩散模块，有效克服了核辐射预报中的非平稳、空间不均和物理耦合三大难题，显著提升预测精度。代码与数据集已公开，支持后续研究与实际应用。

Abstract: Nuclear radiation, the energy released during atomic decay, poses persistent risks to public health and the environment, and concerns have only grown since the Fukushima accident and the recent commencement of treated-water discharge. Modern monitoring networks now record radiation levels and accompanying weather conditions at thousands of stations, opening the door to nationwide forecasting that can inform emergency response, agricultural advisories, and routine public-safety decisions. However, turning this abundance of monitoring data into reliable forecasts is difficult for three reasons. First, the time series at each station are highly non-stationary, shaped by radioactive decay, weather variability, and irregular human interventions. Second, monitoring stations are severely unevenly distributed in space. Roughly 78% of Japan's stations sit in less than 6% of the country, clustered near Fukushima, which breaks the assumptions of standard graph-based models. Third, radiation co-evolves with heterogeneous context such as wind, temperature, and humidity through atmospheric transport processes that purely data-driven models struggle to capture from observations alone. In this study, we introduce NRFormer+, a spatio-temporal Transformer for nationwide nuclear radiation forecasting. NRFormer+ couples non-stationary temporal attention and density-adaptive spatial attention with a new atmospheric diffusion module that estimates how meteorology drives radiation dispersion and injects this physical signal into the network as an architectural prior. NRFormer+ delivers state-of-the-art accuracy on both datasets across all 13 baselines, reducing sudden-change MAE by up to 19.1% over the strongest baseline at comparable inference latency. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/tfeilyu/NRFormer_Plus.

</details>


### [221] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式拓扑优化（GenTO），一个统一的框架，将学习到的拓扑先验转化为可复用的设计引擎。GenTO在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型，然后通过用户定义的物理目标和约束迭代地将拓扑分布引导至任务特定的高性能区域，从而将优化对象从单一结构转变为任务自适应的拓扑分布。在多个拓扑设计问题（热极值、多目标形态控制、目标性能负泊松比设计、振动传输设计）中，GenTO复用预训练拓扑先验处理异构任务，保持结构多样性，并通过数值基准和实验验证达到高性能解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构材料设计方法通常针对单一设计问题定制，对拓扑知识的利用有限，难以在目标、约束和物理功能变化时实现有效且广泛适用的设计。

Method: GenTO首先在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型以学习拓扑先验分布，然后通过用户定义的物理目标和约束，迭代地引导（steer）该拓扑分布朝向任务特定的高性能区域，从而将优化从搜索单一最优结构转变为搜索任务自适应的拓扑分布。

Result: 在热极值设计、多目标形态控制、目标性能负泊松比设计、振动传输设计等多个异构拓扑设计任务中，GenTO成功复用预训练的拓扑先验，保持了结构多样性，并达到了高性能解决方案，通过数值基准和实验验证得到支持。

Conclusion: 可复用的拓扑知识可以作为统一的设计原则，实现有效且可扩展的架构材料设计，GenTO证明了这一点。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints. This shifts the object of optimization from a single structure to a task-adapted topology distribution. Across topology design problems spanning thermal extremization, multi-objective morphology control, property-targeted auxetic design, and vibration transmission design, GenTO reuses pretrained topology priors for heterogeneous tasks, preserves structural diversity, and reaches high-performing solutions supported by numerical benchmarks and experimental validation. These results establish reusable topology knowledge as a unified principle for effective and scalable architected metamaterial design.

</details>


### [222] [HOBA: Hierarchical On-Policy Bidding Agents for Adaptive Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24779)
*Ji Wu,Yunshan Peng,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Wenzheng Shu,Jinan Pang,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HOBA（分层在线竞价智能体），一种分层强化学习框架，通过三层时间尺度解耦竞价中的策略推理、模型选择和出价执行：高层LLM通过思考-行动-观察-反思循环推断超参数，中层SARSA智能体动态选择专家模型并消除选择偏差，底层动态专家池（PID、MPC、IQL、Decision Transformer）执行出价。在线学习被限定在离散的专家选择上，降低探索风险。在AuctionNet基准和线上A/B测试中取得一致改进，上线后目标成本提升+3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线广告竞价系统部署多个离线训练的专家模型（如PID、MPC、离线RL策略），但面临两个关键限制：缺乏对非平稳拍卖市场的在线适应性，以及依赖昂贵的手动超参数调优（如出价边界、预算控制约束）。

Method: HOBA框架包含三个层次：高层使用大语言模型通过Think-Act-Observe-Reflect循环并检索历史经验来推断超参数；中层使用SARSA智能体动态选择专家模型，并引入因果调整消除选择偏差；底层维护一个动态专家池（PID、MPC、IQL、Decision Transformer）在高层次约束下执行出价。该设计将在线学习限制在离散的专家选择上，而非连续出价优化，从而显著降低探索风险同时保持适应性。

Result: 在AuctionNet基准测试和大规模线上A/B测试中，HOBA consistently优于最先进的基线。在线上部署中，HOBA实现了目标成本+3.6%的提升，证明了分层多智能体竞价范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分层多智能体竞价范式通过将在线学习限定在离散的专家选择，有效解决了现有系统缺乏在线适应性和依赖手动调优的问题，在降低探索风险的同时提升了竞价性能，并在实际业务中验证了其商业价值。

Abstract: Online advertising bidding systems typically deploy multiple offline-trained expert models (e.g., PID controllers, model predictive control, offline RL policies) but face two critical limitations: lack of online adaptability to non-stationary auction markets, and reliance on costly manual tuning of hyperparameters such as bid bounds and budget pacing constraints. We propose HOBA (Hierarchical On-policy Bidding Agents), a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that decouples strategic reasoning, model selection, and bid execution across three time scales. At the high level, a large language model infers hyperparameters from contextual signals through a Think-Act-Observe-Reflect loop with historical experience retrieval. At the mid level, a SARSA agent dynamically selects among expert models, incorporating causal adjustment to eliminate selection bias. At the low level, a dynamic expert pool (PID, MPC, IQL, Decision Transformer) executes bids under high-level constraints. This design confines online learning to discrete expert selection rather than continuous bid optimization, significantly reducing exploration risk while maintaining adaptability. Experiments on the AuctionNet benchmark and a large-scale A/B test demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. In a large-scale online deployment, HOBA delivered substantial business value, achieving a +3.6\% increase in target cost, proving the effectiveness of our hierarchical multi-agent bidding paradigm.

</details>


### [223] [LivingArena: Do LLMs Know What Other LLMs Don't? Peer-Probing as Scalable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24780)
*Xingyu Chen,Rui Wang,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingArena 是一个自动化、抗污染的 LLM 评估框架，通过让模型相互提问并利用对手的知识边界来生成动态排行榜，为静态基准测试和人类偏好提供了可扩展的低成本替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿 LLM 面临挑战：静态基准测试存在数据污染和饱和问题，无法区分顶级模型；人类偏好具有主观性。需要一种自动、抗污染且能揭示模型具体失败模式的评估方法。

Method: LivingArena 框架让模型轮流提问，问题需让对手无法正确回答。提问者通过利用对手知识边界获得奖励，回答者成功作答则获奖励。由强模型组成的裁判组验证问题是否包含客观可验证的答案，未通过则惩罚提问者。

Result: 对十个前沿 LLM 的评估产生了稳定的 Elo 排行榜；行为分析表明模型能够识别并利用对手的认知边界；该方法与人类偏好仅弱相关，但能评估事实严谨性和高阶探测能力。

Conclusion: LivingArena 提供了一种可扩展、低成本且抗污染的连续评估方法，超越了静态知识回忆，能够探测模型的高阶认知能力。

Abstract: Evaluating frontier LLMs is challenging: static benchmarks suffer from contamination and saturation -- leaving users unable to distinguish top models and developers blind to specific failure modes -- while human preference is subjective. In this paper, our question is: \emph{Do LLMs know what other LLMs don't? And can we leverage this dynamic for evaluation?} We present \textbf{LivingArena}, an automated, contamination-resistant evaluation framework. In this framework, models take turns proposing questions, aiming to pose items that opponents cannot answer correctly. Questioners are encouraged to actively identify and exploit opponents' knowledge boundaries, receiving rewards when the answerer fails, while the answerer is rewarded otherwise. To ensure questions contain objectively verifiable answers, a judge panel of strong models validates them, penalizing questioners if validation fails. Evaluating ten frontier LLMs, LivingArena yields a stable Elo leaderboard. Our behavioral analyses show that models identify and exploit their peers' cognitive boundaries: self-play and tournament logs indicate that they localize and double down on opponents' weak dimensions. Beyond static knowledge recall, peer probing measures factual rigor and the higher-order ability to probe an opponent's weaknesses, correlating only weakly with human preference and offering a scalable, low-cost approach to continuous evaluation.

</details>


### [224] [Personalization, Personas, and Forecasting in Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24782)
*James Wedgwood,Pratiksha Thaker,Neil Kale,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过世界价值观调查（WVS）测试了不同提示框架（语言基线、用户国家、角色国家、第三人称）对LLM文化对齐的影响。评估了GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-235B在101个WVS问题、13个语言-国家切片上的表现。结果显示，提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素：国家线索会显著改变答案，但并非所有变化都朝向匹配的人类响应分布。第三人称预测在四个托管模型中的三个上产生了最强的定向对齐，而个性化和角色扮演较弱或不稳定。对齐增益集中在宗教性、性别角色和工作导向的物质价值观等显著价值维度上，而制度信任和民主相关问题仍然困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM行为如何通过人类身份提示（如适应用户、角色扮演人群、预测人们如何回答价值问题）被条件化，并探究这些不同提示框架是否可互换，以及它们如何影响模型与文化价值的对齐。

Method: 使用世界价值观调查（WVS）中的101个问题，在13个语言-国家切片上评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-235B。比较了语言基线、用户国家、角色国家和第三人称四种提示框架，共收集21,008个模型响应行。

Result: 提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素；第三人称预测在三个模型中产生最强的定向对齐；个性化和角色扮演较弱或不稳定；对齐增益集中在宗教性、性别角色和工作导向的物质价值观上，而制度信任和民主相关问题仍难以对齐。

Conclusion: 提示框架在文化价值引导中并非可有可无的选择，它既改变模型行为也改变测量到的对齐程度。研究揭示了不同提示策略对文化对齐效果的系统性差异，并指出了LLM在特定价值维度上对齐的局限性。

Abstract: LLM behavior may be conditioned by human identity in several ways: they may be asked to adapt to users, role-play populations, or forecast how people would answer value-laden questions. We test whether these framings are interchangeable using the World Values Survey (WVS). We evaluate GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Qwen3-235B on 101 WVS-derived questions across 13 language-country slices, comparing a language-only baseline with user-country, persona-country, and third-person prompts. Across 21,008 model-response rows, prompt framing is a first-order determinant of cultural alignment: country cues often shift answers substantially, but not all shifts move toward matched human response distributions. Third-person forecasting yields the strongest directional alignment for three of the four hosted models, while personalization and role-play are weaker or less stable. Alignment gains concentrate on salient value dimensions such as religiosity, gender roles, and work-oriented material values, whereas institutional trust and democracy-related questions remain difficult. These results show that prompt framing is not a cosmetic choice in cultural value elicitation; it changes both model behavior and measured alignment.

</details>


### [225] [Unified Semantic Modeling Framework for Large-Scale Job Understanding at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24783)
*Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Jianqiang Shen,Qi Xiao,Benjamin Hoan Le,Wen Pu,Saurabh Gupta,Ran Zhou,Neha Saraf,Alice Leung,Qianqi Shen,Liangjie Hong,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的统一语义建模框架，用于解决LinkedIn中职位理解任务。通过微调开源SLM，结合合成任务和推理轨迹，实现零样本泛化；并引入多适配器架构和属性分组，高效处理多种下游任务。离线评估和在线A/B测试表明性能提升且降低运维复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职位理解是LinkedIn连接人才与机会的核心任务，但构建可扩展、低成本且高性能的系统具有挑战性。现有方法难以同时满足结构化分类和非结构化实体提取的需求，且模型管理复杂。

Method: 首先微调开源SLM，使用精心设计的合成任务（包含推理轨迹）增强零样本泛化能力，同时处理分类和实体提取。然后构建多适配器架构，通过属性分组实现高效的任务特定适配和简化模型管理。

Result: 离线评估和在线A/B测试显示性能显著提升，同时降低了运维复杂性。

Conclusion: 本文为构建工业级文本理解系统提供了实用见解，证明了SLM结合合成数据和多适配器架构在职位理解任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Job understanding is critical to LinkedIn's mission of connecting talent with opportunity. This task involves transforming unstructured and noisy job postings into standardized or derived job attributes that power numerous LinkedIn products. However, building a scalable, cost-efficient, and high-performing job understanding system remains challenging. In this paper, we present a unified semantic modeling framework powered by a small language model (SLM) to address the challenges. We begin by fine-tuning an open-source SLM using a suite of carefully curated synthetic tasks augmented with reasoning traces. These tasks jointly target taxonomy-guided classification and taxonomy-agnostic entity extraction. This allows the resulting model to acquire robust zero-shot generalization for job understanding in structured and unstructured contexts. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a multi-adapter architecture with attribute grouping to facilitate efficient task-specific adaptation while streamlining model management across diverse downstream attributes. Offline evaluations and online A/B tests demonstrate significant performance improvement while reducing operational complexity. Our work provides practical insights into building industry-scale text understanding systems.

</details>


### [226] [On the Use of LLMs for Specialised Terminology: A Good Alternative to Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24784)
*Joachim Minder,Guillaume Wisniewski,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在专业翻译（英译法）中辅助术语查找的能力，评估了GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek四个模型，在地球环境与行星科学（EEPS）和自然语言处理（NLP）两个专业领域，基于每领域80个术语，比较了术语模式和翻译模式两种提示策略。结果显示Claude Sonnet 4.5在最佳配置下表现最好，DeepSeek稳定性最强；模型间、提示策略间存在明显差异，领域间差异较小。置信度估计只能部分反映术语准确性。LLMs可作为专业翻译的有用工具，但尚无法替代专业语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业翻译依赖文档和术语资源（包括语料库），但这些资源的编制和利用存在时间消耗大、技术要求高、数据收集困难等局限性。本研究旨在探索LLMs能否帮助专业译员在英译法过程中查找术语对应词，以克服上述局限性。

Method: 选取四个专有模型（GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5、DeepSeek），在两个专业领域（EEPS和NLP）中各选取80个术语，设计两种提示策略（术语模式和翻译模式），比较不同模型和策略下术语翻译的准确性，并分析模型的置信度估计与术语准确性的关系。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.5在最有利配置下取得最佳结果，DeepSeek稳定性最高。模型之间、提示策略之间存在明显差异，领域间差异较小。置信度估计仅能部分指示术语准确性，并非完全可靠。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为专业翻译的有用辅助工具，但在现阶段还不能替代专业语料库。该研究为未来在实际工作和教育环境中评估LLMs对专业译员的实用价值奠定了基础。

Abstract: Specialised translation relies on the use of documentary and terminological resources, including corpora. These resources are particularly useful for terminology. However, their compilation and exploitation have several limitations: they require time, technical skills and access to data that can be difficult to collect. This study examines the extent to which LLMs can assist specialised translators in finding equivalents from English to French. We evaluate four proprietary models, GPT-4o, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and DeepSeek, in two specialised domains, Earth, Environmental and Planetary Sciences (EEPS) and Natural Language Processing (NLP). The experiment is based on 80 terms per domain and compares two prompting strategies: a terminology and a translation mode. The results highlight clear differences between models, prompting strategies and, to a lesser extent, domains. Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves the best results in the most favourable configuration, while DeepSeek stands out for its greater stability. Analysis of confidence estimates also shows that they are only a partial indicator of terminological accuracy. Overall, the findings suggest that LLMs can be useful tools for specialised translators, but cannot, at this stage, replace specialised corpora. This research therefore paves the way for future work on the real practical usefulness of LLMs for specialised translators in work and educational contexts.

</details>


### [227] [SpecPrefetch: Parameter-Efficient Expert Prefetching for Sparse MoE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24787)
*Jinwei Kong,Runqi Meng,Fanyi Wang,Wentao Qiu,Haotian Hu,Yongjian Zhou,Zhenhua Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SpecPrefetch，一种参数高效预取框架，通过共享轻量适配器预测下一层专家候选并异步加载，解决 MoE 模型推理中因原生 top-K 路由导致的“路由→加载→执行”序列化瓶颈，在提升吞吐量的同时不影响预训练路由语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 MoE 模型虽通过条件专家激活扩展模型容量，但完整专家池在有限加速器内存下难以部署。专家卸载（将非活跃专家移至主机内存或存储）虽缓解内存压力，却引入路由依赖的传输瓶颈：原生 top-K 路由确定所需专家后，推理必须串行执行路由、专家加载和专家执行，导致暴露的加载延迟影响效率。

Method: SpecPrefetch 使用一个共享的轻量适配器（lightweight adapter）来预测下一层的专家候选，仅用于异步传输预取；而冻结的原生路由器仍决定最终执行的专家。通过将传输预测与执行路由分离，SpecPrefetch 减少了暴露的专家加载延迟，且不改变预训练路由语义——预测错误影响传输效率而非模型输出。此外，设计了一个窗口感知调度器，在缓存和带宽约束下优先处理可行传输。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B 和 DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny 上，SpecPrefetch 在 10 个模型-基准设置中的 9 个中取得了最佳平均专家召回率，且可训练参数远少于学习预测基线。在 Snapdragon 8 Elite 设备上，相较计算优化的卸载运行时，解码吞吐量提升高达 20%。

Conclusion: SpecPrefetch 通过解耦预取预测与执行路由，有效降低了 MoE 模型卸载推理的暴露加载延迟，提升了吞吐量，同时保持了预训练路由的语义完整性，为存储受限场景下的 MoE 部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models expand foundation model capacity through conditional expert activation, but their full expert pools remain difficult to deploy under limited accelerator memory. Although expert offloading alleviates memory pressure by moving inactive experts to host memory or storage, it introduces a routing-dependent transfer bottleneck: required experts are known only after native top-\(K\) routing, which serializes routing, expert loading, and expert execution during inference. To address this bottleneck, we propose SpecPrefetch, a parameter-efficient prefetching framework for offloaded MoE inference. SpecPrefetch uses a shared lightweight adapter to predict next-layer expert candidates only for asynchronous transfer, while the frozen native router still determines the final executed experts. By separating transfer prediction from execution routing, SpecPrefetch reduces exposed expert-loading latency without changing pretrained routing semantics, so prediction errors affect transfer efficiency rather than model outputs. In addition, a window-aware scheduler prioritizes feasible transfers under cache and bandwidth constraints. Across Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B and DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, SpecPrefetch achieves the best average expert recall in 9 out of 10 model-benchmark settings with substantially fewer trainable parameters than learned predictor baselines. On a Snapdragon 8 Elite device, SpecPrefetch further improves decoding throughput by up to \(20\%\) over a compute-optimized offloading runtime, demonstrating practical benefits for storage-constrained MoE deployment. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/wei390/SpecPrefetch.

</details>


### [228] [GLIDE: Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24788)
*Vimal William,Ravi Tandon,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIDE提出了一种引导式逐层混合注意力机制，通过在早期层保留滑动窗口softmax注意力、在深层采用线性循环聚合，并引入逐层自适应平衡策略，非均匀地压缩softmax足迹，从而减少长上下文生成中KV缓存的内存I/O和计算开销，同时保持模型表达能力和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型处理越来越长的上下文，解码阶段KV缓存的内存I/O和计算开销成为主要吞吐量瓶颈。现有均匀混合注意力方法未考虑层间异质性，无法在效率和表达能力之间取得最优平衡。

Method: GLIDE基于层间异质性洞察（早期层对去除softmax敏感，深层层存在冗余且可容忍线性替代），引入逐层自适应机制：每层在高效线性循环与可变大小softmax窗口之间平衡，非均匀地压缩整个模型的softmax足迹。

Result: 实验表明GLIDE在性能-效率权衡上优于现有方法，在不损害生成质量的前提下显著降低了长上下文生成端到端延迟。

Conclusion: GLIDE通过引导式逐层混合注意力有效解决了长上下文解码的KV缓存瓶颈，验证了利用层间异质性进行非均匀注意力压缩的可行性，为高效长上下文生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: As Large Language Models scale to increasingly long contexts, the memory I/O and computational overhead of the Key-Value (KV) cache during decoding emerges as the primary throughput bottleneck. To address this, we propose GLIDE, a Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention that strategically integrates sliding-window softmax attention with linear recurrent aggregation. GLIDE is motivated by layer-wise heterogeneity: early layers exhibit high sensitivity to softmax removal, while deeper layers demonstrate redundancy and tolerate aggressive replacement by linear alternatives.
