We ask a complementary question: on a specific, fully reproducible 60M-parameter encoder-decoder model (T5-small) and a single-table text-to-SQL benchmark (WikiSQL), how much task accuracy does each efficiency knob actually cost? We run a controlled, single-variable study over (i) LoRA rank r in {2, 4, 8, 16, 32}, (ii) the set of adapted modules, and (iii) numerical precision. We report task accuracy alongside system-level metrics including trainable parameters, peak training memory, inference latency, and throughput, and frame adaptation as a constrained trade-off rather than an accuracy-only objective. Our results show that LoRA with r=16 recovers within 11.6 percentage points of full fine-tuning accuracy (59.6% vs. 71.2% exact-match) while training fewer than 1% of parameters and consuming 31% less peak GPU memory. Within this setting, rank beyond r=16 yields no measurable accuracy gain. QLoRA with INT8 and NF4 quantization achieves comparable accuracy (52.8% and 53.2%) at dramatically lower memory cost (0.60 GB each), demonstrating a compelling trade-off for memory-constrained deployments. All code, configurations, and logs are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [292] [A Density-Matrix Framework for Electronic-Structure Analysis of Functional-Group and Salt Effects in Lithium-Metal Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25597)
*Mingkang Liu,Huize Yu,Yanbin Gao,Nan Yao,Xiang Chen,Lei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以密度矩阵为中心的AI平台EMolStudio，用于预测和分析锂金属电解质中分子功能团、Li⁺溶剂化壳层和盐阴离子相互作用下的电子结构。该平台通过整合分子功能化、显式Li⁺第一壳层组装、密度矩阵预测及投影等流程，对163,655个功能化分子和22,500个显式Li⁺第一壳层簇进行分析，揭示了功能团对前沿轨道、静电势和Li⁺-供体键序的影响，以及阴离子身份对前沿轨道局域化的重塑作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂金属电解质的反应性源于分子功能团、Li⁺溶剂化和盐阴离子参与之间的相互作用，这种相互作用通过电子密度的重新分布体现。量子化学计算准确但计算成本高，而机器学习电子结构模型很少覆盖化学多样化的溶剂化壳层或电解质相关的读出量。因此，需要一种高效且能处理多样化化学空间的电子结构预测与分析方法。

Method: 提出了一个以密度矩阵为中心的AI平台EMolStudio，其工作流程包括：分子功能化、显式Li⁺第一壳层组装、密度矩阵预测（含幂等投影），以及前沿轨道、静电势、Li⁺-供体键序和电子局域化等读出量的计算。该平台应用于163,655个功能化分子和22,500个显式Li⁺第一壳层簇，涵盖四种锂盐。

Result: 1) 在分子尺度上，功能团（CO₂Me、CN、F/CF₃、磺酰基）通过π*受体、诱导和极化贡献引起前沿能级、静电势和Li⁺-供体接触的化学特异性变化，且在高功能化度下呈亚线性积累；2) 在显式溶剂化壳层中，阴离子身份重塑前沿轨道局域化：LiTDI使HOMO固定于阴离子上，而LiDFOB则使HOMO位于阴离子、LUMO强烈依赖功能团。

Conclusion: EMolStudio能将功能团和盐的选择转化为与锂键形成、去溶剂化和界面反应相关的电子结构假说，为理解锂金属电解质的反应性提供了一种高效的计算工具。

Abstract: The reactivity of lithium-metal electrolytes arises from the interplay of molecular functional groups, Li$^+$ solvation, and salt-anion participation. This interplay operates through the redistribution of electron density across donor, anion, and cation centers, which is most directly read out from the electronic structure resolved in space. Quantum-chemical calculations deliver such readouts faithfully, yet become computationally demanding across this multidimensional design space, and machine-learning electronic-structure models seldom cover chemically diverse solvation shells or electrolyte-relevant readouts. Here, we present a density-matrix-centered AI platform (EMolStudio) for electronic-structure prediction and analysis. Its workflow integrates molecular functionalization, explicit Li$^+$ first-shell assembly, density-matrix prediction with idempotency projection, and readouts of frontier orbitals, electrostatic potential, Li$^+$-donor bond order, and electron localization. We apply EMolStudio to 163,655 functionalized molecules and 22,500 explicit Li$^+$ first-shell clusters across four lithium salts. We find that 1) at the molecular scale, functionalization distinguishes CO$_2$Me, CN, F/CF$_3$, and sulfonyl groups by chemically distinct changes in frontier levels, electrostatic potential, and Li$^+$-donor contact, consistent with $π^*$-acceptor, inductive, and polarization contributions, with sublinear accumulation at higher degrees of functionalization; 2) in explicit solvation shells, anion identity reshapes frontier-orbital localization: LiTDI anchors the HOMO on the anion across the entire library, whereas LiDFOB pairs an anion-hosted HOMO with strongly functional-group-dependent LUMO hosting. EMolStudio thereby translates functional-group and salt choices into electronic-structure hypotheses relevant to lithium-bond formation, desolvation, and interphase reactions.

</details>


### [293] [Computational Extraction of Legal Causes via al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim: A Set-Theoretic Formalization for Closed Fiqh Chapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25605)
*Elnaser Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了古典伊斯兰法学方法论“al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim”（检验与划分）的集合论形式化框架。通过引入一个计算算法，该算法从法学裁决的真值表中提取最小操作规则。主要结果是：给定一个封闭章节的完整真值表，算法能够计算出裁决的最小结构生成器，并消除所有逻辑冗余属性，从而生成可供后续法学评估的候选原因。该框架依赖于有限且学派相对的概念词汇表以及所研究章节的完整裁决表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对古典伊斯兰法学方法论“al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim”（检验与划分）进行形式化描述，以便在封闭的法学章节中提取法律原因（علل）。

Method: 提出一种计算算法，从法学裁决的真值表中提取最小操作规则。该算法基于集合论形式化框架，通过分析真值表来识别最小结构生成器，并消除逻辑冗余属性。

Result: 给定一个封闭章节的完整真值表，算法能够计算出裁决的最小结构生成器，并消除所有逻辑冗余属性，从而生成可供后续法学评估的候选原因。

Conclusion: 该框架的有效性依赖于两个前提条件：一是存在一个有限且学派相对的概念词汇表，二是所研究章节的完整裁决表可用。

Abstract: This paper presents a set-theoretic formalization of the classical usuli method of al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim (Examination and Division) for extracting legal causes ('ilal) within closed chapters of jurisprudence. A computational algorithm is introduced that extracts minimal operational rules from a truth table of juristic verdicts. The principal result is that, given a complete truth table for a closed chapter, the algorithm computes the minimal structural generators of the ruling and eliminates all logically redundant attributes. The resulting structures constitute admissible candidate causes for subsequent juristic evaluation. The framework is conditional upon the availability of a finite school-relative concept vocabulary and a complete ruling table for the chapter under investigation.

</details>


### [294] [Multi-Sensor Alignment for Weather Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25612)
*Samsad Alam,Devyani Lambhate,Aditya Mohan,Vishal Kumar,Vaibhav Katewa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多传感器天气模拟的对齐方法，确保不同传感器间天气特征（强度、粒子位置）一致，并验证了其对3D检测模型鲁棒性的提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知任务需要在恶劣天气下正常工作，但缺乏真实天气数据集；天气模拟是可行的替代方案，但需保证模拟数据在传感器间反映一致的天气特征（如严重程度、粒子定位）。

Method: 提出ReDAM（参考数据集对齐方法）用于雾的天气强度对齐，以及Unified-weather-edit（受Weather-edit启发）用于雨雪中粒子定位对齐；分别通过统计检验和几何检验验证对齐效果。

Result: 未对齐版本的3D检测模型倾向于过度乐观（性能虚高）；使用对齐后的多传感器模拟数据微调现有传感器融合模型，可有效提升3D目标检测任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多传感器天气模拟对齐方法能更真实地反映恶劣天气对感知的影响，并有助于训练更鲁棒的3D检测模型。

Abstract: Perception tasks for autonomous vehicles need to work satisfactorily in adverse weather conditions. Due to lack of real-world weather datasets, weather simulations are a promising alternative. To ensure simulations closely mirror real-world weather data, it's crucial that they represent the same weather characteristics, including severity and particle positioning, across different sensors. To achieve this, we propose the Reference Dataset Alignment Method (ReDAM) for weather intensity alignment in fog and Unified-weather-edit (inspired by Weather-edit[1]) for particle positioning alignment in rain and snow. We validate both alignment methods using statistical and geometrical tests, respectively. We find that 3D detection models for non-aligned versions tend to be overly optimistic as compared to aligned versions. We also show the aligned-multi-sensor simulation's effectiveness for achieving robustness for 3D object detection task by finetuning existing sensor fusion models on it.

</details>


### [295] [Beyond Epistemia: Epistemic Schizologia and Large Language Models as Techno-Semiotic Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25620)
*Federico Cabitza,Gianluca Colombo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文接受Quattrociocchi等人关于LLM造成“Epistemia”（以语言合理性替代认知评估）的诊断，但挑战其将人类认知者与孤立模型对比的解释框架。借鉴Carlo Sini的哲学，作者将LLM理解为自动化书面符号生产环节的“技术-符号机器”，并提出“认知分裂”（epistemic schizologia）这一更广泛现象：语言完成的符号表达与社会嵌入的符号解释、证据、批评、验证、责任回路之间的技术-社会断裂。这一断裂由“似真封闭”（eikotic closure）、算法权威和认知自我误认所强化。因此，相关分析单元不是模型本身，而是包含提示、解释、验证、质疑、使用和产生后果的完整实践。这一重新框架保留了语言生产与负责任理解之间的区分，并奠定了以可追溯谱系、可争议性、分布式责任、认知能动性及人机混合实践评估为核心的设计纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quattrociocchi等人警告LLM的流畅输出可能让语言合理性替代认知评估，产生“Epistemia”状态。本文接受该诊断，但认为其解释框架有缺陷——它通过比较具身、社会嵌入的人类认知者与孤立的生成模型，将认知合法性定位于自主主体的内部能力。作者希望提供一种更好的解释框架，从实践和符号学角度理解LLM与认知的关系。

Method: 借鉴Carlo Sini关于实践、写作、符号和技术（technics）的哲学，提出将LLM理解为“技术-符号机器”（techno-semiotic machine），它通过从人类写作的沉积档案中产生似是而非的语言配置，来自动化书面符号生产的一个阶段。基于此，提出“认知分裂”（epistemic schizologia）概念，并分析其强化机制：似真封闭（eikotic closure）、算法权威、认知自我误认。

Result: 提出认知分裂是比Epistemia更根本的现象，即语言表达符号与社会嵌入的符号回路之间的断裂。识别出强化这一断裂的三种机制：似真封闭（以最终性的认知结果呈现似是而非的延续）、算法权威、认知自我误认。论证相关分析单元不是模型本身，而是包含提示、解释、验证、质疑、使用和产生后果的完整实践。保持了语言生产与负责任理解之间的区分。

Conclusion: 这一重新框架奠定了以可追溯谱系（inspectable genealogy）、可争议性（contestability）、分布式责任（distributed responsibility）、认知能动性（epistemic agency）及人机混合实践评估为核心的设计纲领。强调应将关注点从模型内部能力转向完整的符号实践回路。

Abstract: Quattrociocchi and colleagues warn that the fluent outputs of large language models may allow linguistic plausibility to substitute for epistemic evaluation, producing the condition they call *Epistemia*: the experience of possessing knowledge without undertaking the practices through which judgment would ordinarily be warranted. This article accepts that diagnosis but challenges its explanatory framework, which compares an embodied, socially situated human knower with an isolated generative model thereby locating epistemic legitimacy in capacities internal to autonomous agents. Drawing on Carlo Sini's philosophy of practices, writing, signs, and technics, we propose instead to understand a large language model (LLM) as a *techno-semiotic machine* that automates a phase of written semiosis by producing plausible linguistic configurations from the sedimented archive of human writing. From this perspective, *Epistemia* is one consequence of a broader phenomenon that we call *epistemic schizologia*: the socio-technical cleavage between signs as linguistically accomplished expressions and signs as moments within socially embedded circuits of interpretation, evidence, criticism, verification, and responsibility. This cleavage is reinforced by *eikotic closure*, through which a plausible continuation is presented with the finality of an epistemic result, and by algorithmic authority and epistemic self-misrecognition. The relevant unit is therefore not the model alone but the complete practice in which generated inscriptions are prompted, interpreted, verified, contested, used, and made consequential. This reframing preserves the distinction between linguistic production and responsible understanding while grounding a design programme centred on inspectable genealogy, contestability, distributed responsibility, epistemic agency, and the evaluation of hybrid human--AIpractices.

</details>


### [296] [Quotient Dynamics, Effective Curvature, and Implicit Bias in Positive Quadratic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25624)
*Pengcheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了正二次网络的低秩表示 f_U(x)=x^⊤ UU^⊤ x，其中 U∈ℝ^{d×r} 在右正交乘法下可识别，表示秩为 r 的半正定矩阵 Q=UU^⊤。我们探讨了这种商结构如何控制训练动态、曲率、恢复和插值偏差。我们证明了在满列秩层上，ℝ^{d×r}_*/O(r) 与秩 r 的半正定流形等同。对于光滑目标 L(U)=ℓ(UU^⊤)，欧几里得因子梯度是水平的，因此因子梯度流精确投影到商黎曼梯度流，而有限步梯度下降导出预测器的精确同余递归。对于二次回归，我们推导了插值器处的有效 Hessian 作为限制在切空间上的经验测量 Gram 形式。在高斯秩一测量下，我们计算了总体曲率，证明了经验正态算子的均匀偏差界，构造了谱初始化器，并建立了梯度流的局部指数收敛和小步长下降的线性收敛。恢复保证是显式的但保守，因为依赖于全空间二阶矩控制。在欠定交换机制中，因子梯度流成为联合谱坐标中的精确熵镜像流。严格正初始化收敛到插值集上的 Bregman 投影。各向同性初始化 q(0)=ε^2 𝟙 下，预测器在 ε↓0 时接近最小迹解集，通过不变联合谱代数中的加权熵解决非唯一性。有限步下降选择与连续时间 Bregman 投影相差 O(η) 的插值。数值实验验证了这些商恒等式、曲率预测、恢复行为和选择律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩分解在正二次网络中具有商结构（U 的右正交不变性），但这一结构如何影响训练动力学、曲率、恢复能力和插值偏好尚未被充分理解。本文旨在揭示该商结构在梯度流、梯度下降、Hessian 曲率、恢复保证和插值选择中的核心作用。

Method: 通过将 ℝ^{d×r}_*/O(r) 等同于秩 r 的半正定流形，分析因子梯度流的水平性质，证明其精确投影到商黎曼梯度流；推导有限步梯度下降的精确同余递归；在二次回归中导出有效 Hessian；在高斯秩一测量下计算总体曲率、建立经验正态算子偏差界、构造谱初始化器并证明收敛性；在欠定交换机制中，利用联合谱坐标将因子梯度流转化为熵镜像流，分析 Bregman 投影和最小迹解的选择。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 因子梯度流精确投影到商黎曼梯度流，梯度下降诱导精确同余递归；(2) 二次回归中有效 Hessian 为限制切空间上的经验 Gram 矩阵；(3) 高斯秩一测量下，获得显式但保守的恢复保证，梯度流局部指数收敛，小步长下降线性收敛；(4) 欠定交换机制中，因子梯度流等价于熵镜像流，严格正初始化收敛到 Bregman 投影，各向同性初始化在极限下选择最小迹解；(5) 有限步下降与连续时间 Bregman 投影的差异为 O(η)。

Conclusion: 本文系统揭示了正二次网络中低秩表示的商结构对训练动力学、曲率、恢复和插值偏差的深刻影响，为理解此类网络的优化行为和归纳偏差提供了理论基础，数值实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Positive quadratic networks admit the low-rank representation f_U(x)=x^top UU^top x, where Uinmathbb{R}^{dtimes r} is identifiable only up to right orthogonal multiplication, representing a rank-r PSD matrix Q=UU^top. We study how this quotient structure governs training dynamics, curvature, recovery, and interpolation bias. On the full-column-rank stratum, we identify mathbb{R}^{dtimes r}_*/O(r) with the rank-r PSD manifold. For smooth objectives L(U)=ell(UU^top), the Euclidean factor gradient is horizontal. Thus, factor gradient flow projects exactly to quotient Riemannian gradient flow, while finite-step gradient descent induces an exact congruence recursion for the predictor. For quadratic regression, we derive the effective Hessian at interpolators as the empirical measurement Gram form restricted to the tangent space relative to the quotient metric. Under Gaussian rank-one measurements, we compute population curvature, prove uniform deviation bounds for the empirical normal operator, construct a spectral initializer, and establish local exponential convergence for gradient flow and linear convergence for small-step descent. Recovery guarantees are explicit but conservative due to reliance on full-space second-moment control. In underdetermined commuting regimes, factor gradient flow becomes an exact entropy mirror flow in joint spectral coordinates. Strictly positive initializations converge to Bregman projections onto the interpolation set. With isotropic initialization q(0)=varepsilon^2mathbf{1}, predictors approach the minimum-trace solution set as varepsilondownarrow0, resolving nonuniqueness via weighted entropy within the invariant joint spectral algebra. Finite-step descent selects interpolants differing from continuous-time Bregman projections by O(eta). Numerical experiments verify these quotient identities, curvature predictions, recovery behaviors, and selection laws.

</details>


### [297] [Joint Text-Audio Alignment for EEG-to-Text Decoding in Chinese Speech Production and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25626)
*Tian Zheng,Xurong Xie,Xinxin Zhu,Xiaolan Peng,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EEGAlign框架，通过双轴对齐（文本语义和音频声学特征）和对比学习+CTC解码，从非侵入性EEG信号中解码中文句子，在ChineseEEG-2数据集上达到82.37%（朗读）和41.43%（被动聆听）的Top-1准确率，是首个在非侵入性EEG上实现大词汇量中文句子解码的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严重言语和运动障碍患者提供非侵入性神经通信通路；中文句子解码面临高维输出空间、受试者间差异大、信噪比低等挑战；现有方法仅依赖单一监督轴（文本语义或音频声学特征），无法同时满足句子级判别力和细粒度时间分辨率的需求。

Method: 提出EEGAlign，一个参数高效的框架，通过对比学习将EEG与两个轴对齐：文本轴使用BGE-M3文本嵌入，音频轴使用wav2vec 2.0语音特征；随后采用CTC字符序列解码。在ChineseEEG-2数据上进行封闭集句子分类。

Result: 在ChineseEEG-2数据集上取得最先进结果：朗读EEG Top-1准确率82.37%，被动聆听EEG Top-1准确率41.43%（101个候选句子）。消融实验表明双轴对齐具有高度互补性，联合使用优于任一单独轴。

Conclusion: 这是首次在非侵入性EEG上（发声言语生产期间）解码大词汇量中文句子的研究，在较大封闭集候选句子设定下取得了强分类性能，证明了双轴对齐的有效性和互补性。

Abstract: Decoding speech information directly from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) into text provides a potential non-invasive neural communication pathway for individuals with severe speech and motor impairments. Compared with invasive approaches such as electrocorticography, EEG is safer and more widely deployable, yet substantially more challenging to decode.This challenge is exacerbated for Chinese sentence decoding, which must handle a high-dimensional output space with thousands of characters, severe inter-subject variability, and low signal-to-noise ratios for text alignment.Existing methods commit to a single supervisory axis---either text semantics or audio acoustic features---yet neither can simultaneously satisfy the demands of sentence-level discriminability and fine-grained temporal resolution required for large-vocabulary Chinese decoding. We introduce EEGAlign, a novel parameter-efficient framework that jointly aligns EEG with two axes---text alignment with BGE-M3 text embeddings and audio alignment with wav2vec~2.0 speech features via contrastive learning followed by CTC character-sequence decoding. On ChineseEEG-2 data, EEGAlign yields state-of-the-art closed-set sentence classification performance, reaching up to 82.37% Top-1 accuracy on Reading Aloud EEG and 41.43% on Passive Listening EEG out of 101 candidates. Ablation studies show that the two alignment axes are highly complementary: combining them yields consistently better performance than either alone. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study on decoding large-vocabulary Chinese sentences from non-invasive EEG during overt speech production, and achieving strong classification performance with relatively large closed-set candidate-sentence setting.

</details>


### [298] [Engine-Equal, Human-Unequal: A Reproducible Outcome Skew in Engine-Assessed Equal Chess Positions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25655)
*Jesung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在引擎（Stockfish 18）评估为均势（±10 centipawns）且人类实际对局中出现的1,661个开局局面（来自Lichess 2025年10月数据，共1,610万对局）中，人类对局结果并非平衡的。每个局面存在结果偏差（实际结果与等级分预测结果之差），且偏差方向是局面的稳定属性：有些局面有利白方，有些有利黑方。这些偏差在三个不同划分（玩家账号集合、时间、等级分段）及八个月后的外样本月份中均可复现。主要划分中，每个局面的偏差在两个账号组中各测量一次，复制斜率（去除等级分和开局家族效应后）为0.69（按家族聚类95% CI [0.65, 0.74]），在最流行的局面上升至0.94。斜率值取决于局面构成。存在性是不变主张：所有更紧的评估带宽、搜索深度、校准和流行度截断下均成立，并在快棋和超快棋中分别复现。典型偏差较小（中位数 |δ| ≈ 0.018，约白方得分的两个百分点），但仍逐局面地在不交叉的账号间复现。在这些局面中，不利方思考时间更长。即使评估最确信时，它也不是人类结果的充分统计量。该结果为观察性，因果问题留给预注册的随机对照研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在引擎评估为绝对均势的开局局面中，人类对局结果是否真的平衡，还是存在系统性的偏好（某些局面天然有利白方或黑方），从而挑战“引擎评估等同于人类实际结果”的隐含假设。

Method: 基于Lichess 2025年10月数据，筛选出Stockfish 18评估在±10 centipawns以内、深度稳定的1,661个开局局面（共1,610万对局）。定义结果偏差δ为实际结果与基于玩家等级分预测结果的差值。通过三个划分（主划分：不交叉的玩家账号集合；时间划分；等级分段划分）及八个月后的外样本月份验证偏差的复现性。使用复制斜率度量同一局面在两个账号组中偏差测量值的预测关系（去除等级分和开局家族效应），并进行多种稳健性检验（更紧评估带宽、搜索深度、校准、流行度截断、分时限类别）。

Result: 主划分复制斜率为0.69（按开局家族聚类95% CI [0.65, 0.74]），最流行局面升至0.94。所有稳健性检验下偏差存在性均成立。典型偏差绝对值中位数约0.018（约白方得分2个百分点）。不利方平均思考时间更长。

Conclusion: 引擎评估即使是深度稳定的均势评估，也不是人类对局结果的充分统计量；人类结果存在系统性的、可复现的局面偏差。该结论为观察性，因果机制需通过预注册随机对照研究进一步探究。

Abstract: Among chess opening positions that a strong engine judges essentially equal (Stockfish 18 evaluation within 10 centipawns of zero, depth-stable) and that humans actually reach on Lichess (October 2025; 1,661 positions, 16.1M occurrences), human results are not balanced. Positions carry outcome skews, each the gap between its games' actual results and what the players' ratings predict, whose directions are stable properties of the naturally-reached position: some positions favour White, others Black. These skews reproduce across three re-partitions -- disjoint player-account sets (primary), time, and disjoint rating bands -- and on an out-of-sample month eight months later. On the primary split, each position's skew is measured once in each account group, and the replication slope asks how well one measurement predicts the other after removing rating and opening-family effects: one means undiminished carry-over; zero, no linear relation. We find 0.69 (family-clustered 95% CI [0.65, 0.74]), rising to 0.94 on the most-popular, best-measured positions. The slope's value depends on the position mix. Existence is the invariant claim: it survives every tighter evaluation band, search depth, calibration, and popularity cutoff we test, and replicates within blitz and rapid separately. The typical skew is small (median $|δ| \approx 0.018$, about two percentage points of White score), yet it reproduces, position by position, across disjoint accounts. At these positions the disfavoured side also thinks longer. Even where the evaluation is most confident, it is not a sufficient statistic for human outcomes. The result is observational, and the causal question is left to a pre-registered randomised companion study.

</details>


### [299] [OrchBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Plans in Isolation via Deterministic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25656)
*Zhenzhen Ren,Jiyan He,Xinpeng Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Ke Han,Shuxin Zheng,GuoBiao Li,Xiaoqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchBench 是一个基于模拟的基准，用于隔离评估多智能体系统中的编排计划质量，通过有向无环图（DAG）表示任务依赖，并引入模拟器在不实际执行 worker 智能体的情况下评估计划，从而大幅降低评估成本，同时保持与真实执行的高度相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖端到端执行，这会将编排计划质量与 worker 能力、工具可靠性和环境噪声混在一起，且真实执行的时间和 token 成本随工作流规模快速增长，导致系统评估成本高昂。因此需要一种能在隔离条件下高效评估编排计划质量的方法。

Method: 从真实任务构建有向无环图（DAG）编码任务依赖，控制规模和并行度。给定 DAG、每智能体上下文限制和智能体预算，评估的规划器将子任务分配给智能体，并指定跨智能体信息传输及其保留比例。确定性模拟器在不调用 worker 智能体的情况下评估计划，输出结果质量、完成时间和 token 成本的可解释度量。

Result: 模拟得分与 Claude Code 执行的质量得分高度相关（Pearson r=0.816），仅需 1.3% 的 token 和 10.3% 的墙钟时间。实验发现保留任务关键信息比增加智能体数量更重要，且并行收益会随协调失败累积而递减。

Conclusion: OrchBench 建立了一个高效且可解释的基准，用于比较和诊断多智能体编排计划，揭示了编排中信息保留与协调失败的关键作用。

Abstract: Complex tasks often decompose into parallelizable yet interdependent subtasks, making orchestration critical to the performance of multi-agent systems (MAS). Existing evaluations typically rely on end-to-end execution, which conflates orchestration-plan quality with worker capabilities, tool reliability, and environmental noise. Moreover, the time and token costs of real execution grow rapidly with workflow scale, making systematic evaluation expensive. We present OrchBench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating multi-agent orchestration plans in isolation. Starting from real-world tasks, OrchBench constructs directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that encode task dependencies, with controlled sizes and degrees of parallelism. Given a DAG, a per-agent context limit, and an agent budget, the evaluated planner assigns subtasks to agents and specifies cross-agent information transfers and their retention ratios. A deterministic simulator evaluates the resulting plan without invoking worker agents and returns interpretable measures of result quality, makespan, and token cost. The simulated scores produced by OrchBench correlate strongly with quality scores from Claude Code executions, achieving a Pearson correlation of \(r=0.816\), while requiring only \(1.3\%\) of the tokens and \(10.3\%\) of the wall-clock time. Across diverse planners and workflow scales, we find that preserving task-critical information is more important than simply increasing the number of agents, and the benefits of parallelism diminish as coordination failures accumulate. These results establish OrchBench as an efficient and interpretable benchmark for comparing and diagnosing multi-agent orchestration plans.

</details>


### [300] [CoRT: Counterfactual Replay for Token-Level Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25659)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Wen Wang,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoRT 是一种基于反事实回放的 token 级信用分配方法，用于 rubric 条件化的 GRPO 训练。它通过对比有/无 rubric 提示下的 log-likelihood 差异生成 token 级权重，将响应级优势信号重新分配到各个 token，无需辅助评分模型即可提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GRPO 风格管线中，基于 rubric 的结构化评估被压缩为标量响应级奖励，再转换为响应级优势信号均匀广播到所有 token。这导致不同准则（不同跨度、格式选择、语义选择）对最终奖励的贡献无法在 token 层级显式区分，缺乏细粒度信用分配机制。

Method: CoRT 通过反事实回放（counterfactual replay）对同一个采样响应分别在有 rubric 条件提示和无准则提示下重新计算 log-likelihood，计算 token 级的 log-likelihood 对比作为对 rubric 上下文依赖程度的代理。将这些对比映射到有界、响应归一化的权重，用于重新分配带符号的 GRPO 优势信号到各个 token，无需引入额外评分模型或改变响应级奖励。

Result: 在多种指令微调模型和奖励粒度上的实验表明，CoRT 在绝大多数比较中优于匹配的响应级 GRPO，平均提升 4.4 个百分点。与需要额外学习的 token 级信用分配基线相比，CoRT 保持竞争力且无需独立的相关性学习阶段。

Conclusion: 策略内部的反事实似然对比能够为响应内的信用分配提供有效的训练信号，同时保留 GRPO 的简洁性和稳定性。这表明无需外部标注或辅助模型，仅通过对比有无 rubric 条件下的生成概率差异即可实现细粒度奖励分配。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning enriches language model training by evaluating model outputs against explicit criteria. Yet in GRPO-style pipelines, these structured judgments are reduced to a scalar response-level reward and converted into a response-level advantage, which is broadcast uniformly to all generated tokens. This leaves no explicit mechanism for allocating credit within a response, even when different criteria are grounded in different spans, formatting decisions, or semantic choices. We propose CoRT, a token-level credit weighting method for rubric-conditioned GRPO. Instead of training an auxiliary token scoring model, CoRT uses counterfactual replay to rescore the same sampled response under the original rubric-conditioned prompt and a matched criteria-free prompt. The resulting tokenwise log-likelihood contrasts serve as a proxy for dependence on the rubric context. CoRT maps these contrasts to bounded, response-normalized weights and uses them to redistribute the signed GRPO advantage across tokens, without introducing an auxiliary scorer or changing the response-level reward. Experiments across instruction-tuned models and reward granularities show that CoRT improves over matched response-level GRPO in the vast majority of comparisons, with an average gain of 4.4 percentage points. The method remains competitive with learned token-level credit baselines while avoiding a separate relevance-learning stage. These results suggest that policy-internal counterfactual likelihood contrasts provide an effective training signal for within-response credit allocation while retaining the simplicity and stability of GRPO.

</details>


### [301] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmniDelta，一个训练无关的框架，用于在OmniLLMs中跨音频和视频模态进行预算分配，通过意图感知的跨模态分配和内容感知的模态内分配，在保持准确性的同时降低内存和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OmniLLMs中长音频-视频令牌序列导致高内存和推理成本。现有压缩方法关注固定预算下的令牌选择，忽略了跨模态预算分配问题。直接查询到模态的相似度不可靠，且均匀的模态内预算会遗漏关键证据、保留冗余内容。

Method: 提出OmniDelta，训练无关、技能驱动的框架。首先构建音频和视频技能池，根据查询需求转移固定保留令牌预算；然后利用局部复杂度和时间冗余，在音频片段和视频帧上重新分配模态预算。生成的局部预算可与现有剪枝策略结合，保留总令牌比例同时改变预算分配。

Result: 在四个音频-视频基准测试上使用两个Qwen2.5-Omni模型进行实验，OmniDelta建立了新的精度-效率帕累托前沿。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上保留25%令牌时，GPU内存减少22.0%，端到端速度提升1.64倍。

Conclusion: OmniDelta有效解决了预算分配问题，通过智能的跨模态和模态内预算分配，在保持准确性的同时显著提升效率，为OmniLLMs的压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [302] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DecoEvo（解耦共同进化），一种无需使用黄金标准评估集即可在文本空间优化大语言模型的方法。DecoEvo 将求解器技能与评估准则生成器技能解耦并共同进化，求解器基于准则级反馈更新，评估器通过独立于求解器总分的互补审计进行修正，避免了传统文本空间优化中固定评估或共同进化时评估标准被求解器分数误导的问题。实验表明，在五个基准测试和三个LLM骨干上，DecoEvo 的平均性能相对 SkillOpt 提升 2.8-5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间优化方法通常固定评估标准，在开放式任务中，一旦求解器在评估指标上取得高分，未涵盖的维度便无法得到优化信号；而简单地将评估标准与求解器共同进化也不可靠，因为求解器分数提升可能仅仅是因为评估标准变宽松了。因此需要一种解耦机制，使评估标准能持续暴露求解器的新弱点，避免重复优化已满足的准则。

Method: DecoEvo 将求解器（solver skill）和评估准则生成器（rubric-generator skill）作为两个独立的文本空间技能，在解耦目标下共同进化。求解器根据准则级反馈（criterion-level feedback）更新，而非仅仅依赖总分；评估准则生成器通过两种互补审计（requirement coverage 和 response discrimination）进行修订，这两种审计独立于求解器的聚合得分，从而使生成器的更新聚焦于求解器新暴露的弱点，减少对已满足准则的重复强调。

Result: 在五个基准测试（覆盖不同任务）和三个LLM骨干（backbone）上，DecoEvo 在所有对比方法中取得最佳性能，相比 SkillOpt 平均相对提升 2.8-5.0%。实验使用每个基准测试的官方评估标准进行最终评估。

Conclusion: 解耦共同进化是一种有效的文本空间优化策略，通过分离求解器与评估器的更新信号，避免了固定评估的瓶颈和共同进化中的分数欺骗问题，显著提升了LLM在开放式任务上的适应性能。

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [303] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了桌面增量基准（DDB），一个离线步骤级基准，包含2,013个人工验证的实例，用于评估计算机使用代理（CUA）在桌面GUI环境中重建动作因果、任务相关转换的能力，填补了从GUI基础定位到最终任务成功之间的诊断空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只衡量最终任务成功或单帧基础定位，无法判断模型是否能重建动作产生的因果、任务相关的转换（即观察前后状态的变化），这对于拒绝陈旧观察、验证进展和从失败中恢复至关重要。由于推理、远程输入、应用渲染和截图捕获是异步的，下一个观察可能延迟、遮挡、瞬态或无关，导致错误地将虚假状态视为进展并带入后续规划。

Method: DDB是一个离线步骤级基准，包含来自新颖的多应用Linux轨迹的2,013个人工验证实例，覆盖约15个应用和50个任务领域。针对状态验证、源跟踪和上下文感知控制三个失败维度，设计了两个互补任务：463个3帧时序排序实例（其中105个包含跨轨迹诱饵）和1,550个前后对比对，标注了5种动作及其载荷。

Result: 评估了8个闭源和开源模型族在32个排序和16个单动作设置上的表现，发现排序任务尚未饱和：最佳非诱饵和诱饵精确匹配率分别为65.1%和65.7%。任务上下文使诱饵识别提高6.9个百分点，但非诱饵精确匹配下降2.2个百分点；错误分析显示模型系统性地复制给出的A-B-C顺序。单动作结果显示推断动作类别比定位更难：点击的F1为0.96，拖拽为0.76，但识别出的拖拽通常定位良好。

Conclusion: DDB通过填补GUI基础定位和最终任务成功之间缺失的诊断层，补充了端到端基准，能够有针对性地改进桌面CUA的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


### [304] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognivia，一个基于证据的AI治疗师，能够自动识别认知扭曲并生成理性回应，在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在心理健康应用中缺乏领域特异性、回应过于奉承，且缺少认知扭曲的明确定义和标注，限制了大规模应用CBT的效果。

Method: 基于权威CBT文本和心理健康QA数据，采用多阶段提示和结构化生成策略，在行为科学专家监督下微调轻量级LLM得到Cognivia；同时提出首个分层质量评估框架。

Result: 在词汇指标、LLM评判（两个互补标准）和10位行为科学专家人工评估中，Cognivia在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成上持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Cognivia作为证据驱动的AI治疗师，有效提升了认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成能力，有望缓解专业治疗师短缺问题，推动CBT大规模应用。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [305] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper explores how sustainability information framed in recommendation explanations can nudge user choices. Using generative AI to create theory-grounded explanations, two studies (N=529) across low and high involvement domains show that merely disclosing sustainability info does not change behavior, but framing or invoking descriptive social norms significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Perception and behavior diverge: plain disclosure improves evaluations but not choices. The work demonstrates LLMs' potential for scalable, explanation-based interventions for social good.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption and can encourage sustainable choices. Explanations influence users' perceptions and can support informed decisions. The authors argue that explanations can serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions.

Method: The authors use generative AI to generate sustainability-aware explanations based on nudge theory, validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. They then conduct two randomized studies (N=529) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings). Participants choose among preference-matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations.

Result: Across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Perception and behavior diverge: plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior.

Conclusion: LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. The authors conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [306] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 概念瓶颈模型通过可解释的中间变量（左心室容积）预测射血分数，但仅用概念预测准确度评判模型有效性可能不足：当训练目标仅关注射血分数（比率）时，模型在概念层（容积）上失去了物理尺度，因为射血分数对容积缩放不变；额外监督容积绝对值可改善尺度，但会略微增加射血分数误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究概念瓶颈模型的有效性评判标准是否充分：通常以概念预测准确度来评判，但该研究质疑这种评判是否足够，因为训练目标可能具有不变性（如射血分数是比率，对容积缩放不变），导致概念层失去物理尺度。

Method: 使用视频变压器编码器在公开超声心动图数据集上训练，以左心室收缩末期和舒张末期容积作为概念层，通过解析公式计算射血分数（无残差路径）。比较仅用射血分数目标训练与额外监督容积绝对值（毫升）训练的效果，在1276个保留研究上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈未显著增加射血分数误差（MAE 6.89 vs 7.13）。但无容积监督时，预测容积的离散度坍缩至0.1毫升（参考离散度35.7和45.7毫升），相关性部分保留。这是由于射血分数目标对容积缩放不变，导致损失仅确定概念层至尺度。额外容积监督将容积误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，但射血分数误差增加0.4。

Conclusion: 概念准确度可能掩盖概念层缺乏物理尺度的问题；临床模型中的可解释中间变量应针对训练目标的不变性结构进行验证，而不仅依赖预测准确度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [307] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自推测智能体（Self-Speculating Agent），将智能体与推测器统一到同一模型中，通过联合强化学习从智能体自身轨迹中学习预测下一工具调用，在保持任务成功率的同时显著提升工具调用预测准确率（Hit@1），从而减少等待延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在等待工具调用结果时存在大量空闲时间；现有工具调用推测方法通常使用独立的草稿模型或缓存轨迹，与部署的智能体行为不一致，导致推测效果不佳。

Method: 提出自推测智能体：同一模型兼具智能体模式（解决问题）和推测器模式（根据部分轨迹预测下一工具调用），完全复用前缀 KV 缓存；并设计联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身的 rollout 中导出推测目标，交替更新智能体和推测器。

Result: 在智能体搜索 QA 和对话式工具使用任务中，Qwen3-4B 的下一工具调用 Hit@1 从 44.1 提升至 61.2，Qwen3.5-4B 从 48.9 提升至 66.3，且不损害任务成功率。

Conclusion: 自推测智能体通过统一模型设计有效降低了工具调用延迟，为构建更高效的语言模型智能体系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [308] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper addresses large-scale DCOPs by combining online learning algorithms (adapted from potential games) with a novel decomposition framework that splits a DCOP into a meta-DCOP for task allocation and local scheduling subproblems, coupled via iterative pricing. Applied to decentralized satellite scheduling, it achieves over 99% observation request fulfillment, outperforming baselines (87%).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many real-world DCOP instances are too large to solve monolithically; existing incomplete algorithms may not scale. The need for efficient distributed decision making under limited communication, particularly in decentralized satellite scheduling, motivates a new approach.

Method: Two complementary directions: (1) revisiting the connection between DCOPs and potential games, adapting modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs; (2) proposing a decomposition framework that separates a DCOP into a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation and independent local optimization problems for scheduling, coupled via an iterative pricing method that updates meta-level utilities using feedback from local optimizers.

Result: The online learning algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. Combining them with the iterative pricing framework yields near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared to 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

Conclusion: The proposed combination of online learning and iterative pricing decomposition effectively scales DCOP methods to large real-world problems, demonstrated by significant improvements in satellite scheduling performance.

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [309] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill 是一个层次化技能图框架，将 LLM 智能体的交互轨迹组织为包含技能节点、原子操作节点和有向边（分解、时序转换、兼容性、支持、恢复关系）的有向图，在推理时检索任务相关子图并引导智能体执行，从而弥合高层技能与可执行动作之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常产生扁平的、独立存储的高层文本技能集合，未充分利用技能之间的关系，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在差距，导致智能体在长周期交互任务中难以高效复用过往经验。

Method: 提出 HiSkill 框架，构建层次化技能图：(1) 将交互轨迹组织成有向图，包含技能节点（高层技能）和 AtomicOp 节点（可执行动作模板），边类型包括分解、时序转换、兼容性、支持和恢复关系；(2) 推理时检索与任务相关的紧凑子图；(3) 基于符号任务状态、当前活跃技能和检索到的子图，迭代引导 LLM 智能体进行技能切换、AtomicOp 选择以及可执行动作的接地（grounding）。

Result: 在三个交互环境上的实验表明，HiSkill 优于现有最先进基线方法，同时降低了推理阶段的 token 消耗。

Conclusion: 通过层次化技能图桥接高层技能与可执行动作接地是有效的，能够提升 LLM 智能体在长周期交互任务中的表现和效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [310] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在精算风险建模中的不确定性量化问题，提出一个中心枢纽协调下的多智能体框架，通过令牌级对数概率与贝叶斯网络实现不确定性传播，在保持基线精算性能的同时提供工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流是高风险的决策支持场景，LLM的概率性本质和智能体间的依赖关系引入了不可靠输出的风险，可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和监管不合规。因此需要专门处理这些不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，各专业智能体在中心枢纽协调下分别执行数据准备、建模、审查和解释任务。主要创新是使用令牌级对数概率和贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。对数概率不被视为正确性或任务成功的直接概率，而是先转化为长度归一化的对数概率摘要，再转化为校准的任务级置信度估计，最后纳入贝叶斯网络。

Result: 该框架能够复现基线精算性能，同时提供关于工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统可以支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，通过所提出的不确定性传播方法在保持性能的同时增强了工作流的可解释性和可靠性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [311] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了SHarD（Secure Harness Distribution），一个基于Pi agent harness构建的可分发安全控制框架，能够将OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制嵌入商业AI编码智能体，并通过单条安装命令分发，在OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications的23项测试中达到100%调整后得分，与最佳安全配置的商业智能体持平，且无任何回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体正以前所未有的速度被采用，但安全与风险问题仍是其规模化部署的主要障碍。现有安全控制未系统性地覆盖工程团队，而供应商原生的解决方案引入了生态依赖，不一定适应所有部署场景。因此，研究能否在商业AI编码智能体上实现现成的安全控制，并通过自定义智能体框架（harness）扩展到分布式用户群体。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，对四种智能体配置（两种商业智能体分别带与不带安全控制、一个基准框架、一个安全强化框架）进行比较。测试套件包含23项测试，源自OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications。SHarD基于Pi agent harness构建，嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制，通过单条安装命令分发。

Result: SHarD实现了100%的调整后得分，与最佳安全配置的商业智能体性能一致，所有测试类别均无回归。关键发现包括：模型非确定性会导致安全结果不一致；自主智能体行为可能跨越系统边界，而OS沙箱能直接缓解此类风险。

Conclusion: 现成的安全控制可以被嵌入商业AI编码智能体并通过自定义框架分发，SHarD证明了该方法的有效性。研究提出了控制框架适应性评估的初步特征，并识别出未来研究的第三个研究问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [312] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier 是一个统一的 AI Agent 评估语料库，包含 957,253 条记录，覆盖 30 个基准、714 个智能体、11,891 个任务和 74,205 个验证器。它通过标准化记录、跨领域补充运行和反事实重评分，揭示了不同基准类型上的进展不均衡（函数调用饱和、编程提升最快、企业工作流最具挑战性），并指出严格的全通过聚合可能掩盖进展并人为改变排名。从标准化记录中推导出的能力量表与 Epoch 评估能力指数排名高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.81）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI Agent 评估在交互环境中存在碎片化问题：不同任务、脚手架、验证器和评分规则导致结果难以比较。已有工作局限于狭窄设置、规模有限或需要昂贵的重新运行，使得大量实证记录无法相互比较。因此，需要一个统一的、标准化的评估基础设施来系统性地分析 Agent 能力进展。

Method: 构建 Messier 语料库：(1) 整合公开的基准测试分数；(2) 补充六个代表性不足的专业和科学领域（包括最近的法律基准）的五种 Agent 运行结果；(3) 对每条记录按模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则进行标准化；(4) 使用 SOC/NAICS 分类进行职业和行业分析；(5) 进行反事实重评分以评估不同聚合规则的影响；(6) 从标准化记录中推导出能力量表。

Result: (1) 前沿进展在不同基准类型上不均衡：函数调用已饱和，编程提升最快，企业工作流最具挑战性；(2) 严格的全通过聚合在多验证器任务中会掩盖进展并人为改变 Agent 排名；(3) 从标准记录中推导的能力量表与 Epoch 的评估能力指数排名 Spearman ρ=0.81 高度一致；(4) 语料库可按领域、职业、动作空间或验证器类型进行专门化分析。

Conclusion: Messier 提供了一个基础性的、可复用的基础设施，用于 Agent 能力缩放分析、基准审计以及评估失败案例的细粒度分析。它解决了评估碎片化问题，使得跨不同设置的系统性比较成为可能，并揭示了当前评估实践中的关键缺陷（如聚合规则的影响）。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [313] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于"提出-验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体（IRA），用于GUI任务评估。IRA在GUI智能体执行后，通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具，从执行后环境中获取并验证证据，从而判断任务是否完成。同时提出了包含321个GUI任务轨迹的基准GUI-RewardBench。实验表明IRA在基准上达到86.9%的准确率，并可用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练，提升了OSWorld任务成功率至34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的GUI任务评估具有挑战性，因为判断往往需要访问环境状态（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置），而这超出了执行轨迹截图的范畴。现有的自动化评估方法难以获取这些隐藏状态信息，导致评估不准确。

Method: 提出交互式奖励智能体（IRA），基于"提出-验证"框架：首先根据任务指令和GUI环境提出任务完成条件，然后通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具交互式地验证这些条件，从而结合可见界面和环境状态的双重证据进行评估。

Result: 在包含321个GUI任务轨迹的GUI-RewardBench基准上，IRA达到86.9%的准确率，优于现有评估基线。将IRA应用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练后，OSWorld任务成功率提升至34.0%。

Conclusion: IRA通过交互式获取环境状态证据的方式，显著提升了GUI任务评估的可靠性，并且能够为GUI智能体的强化学习训练提供有效的奖励信号，展示了其在GUI智能体自动化评估和训练中的实用价值。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [314] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentToolMO，一个基于3GPP NRM的信息模型，用于多厂商自治网络中AI代理工具的跨厂商信任管理，通过形式化的信任状态机、阻尼级联传播和现有MnS接口的通知机制，实现信任降级的近实时感知与级联收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治网络L4-L5要求AI代理在无人工监督下跨厂商调用工具，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可视化的标准化机制。当厂商B的工具被攻陷时，厂商A的代理仍持续调用该工具，无法感知信任降级，导致级联服务影响。

Method: 提出AgentToolMO信息模型，包含四部分：1) 形式化定义的信任状态机，具备可证明的渐进式强制策略；2) 阻尼级联传播机制，保证有界收敛；3) 通过现有管理服务(MnS)接口实现跨厂商信任通知；4) 基于NRM依赖图遍历的追溯性影响评估。

Result: 仿真评估表明，标准化的跨厂商通知将爆炸半径从小时级未检测传播缩减至近实时遏制（受限于MnS通知投递延迟），级联收敛在有限迭代内保证完成，通知跨厂商域的扩展呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架运行于现有3GPP管理基础设施内，利用已有协议，为可信的多厂商自治网络管理提供了标准化路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [315] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope is a latent-reasoning framework for decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval, using time-modulated GRU dynamics and a CoT-to-latent curriculum to achieve efficient reasoning with reduced inference latency while maintaining competitive accuracy on structured-reasoning benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current language models handle complex structured reasoning by either scaling parameters (costly) or serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens, which ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. Penelope aims to reduce repeated full-decoder execution and avoid generating long visible reasoning traces by shifting reasoning into latent space.

Method: Penelope localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace.

Result: On open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency.

Conclusion: Latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace, providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [316] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了dtControl2的扩展版本"ε"，通过在马尔可夫决策过程中允许一定的精度损失ε≥0，构建更小、更易理解的决策树控制器，在保证ε-最优性的同时大幅简化解释复杂度，生成的决策树比现有技术小几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策树控制器表示方法（如dtControl2）对于大规模系统或具有许多边界情况的系统往往过于复杂，难以被人类理解。直接减小决策树规模可能导致遗漏关键情况而产生错误控制器，因此需要一种可控的简化方法。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的背景下，扩展dtControl2工具，引入ε功能：允许用户指定一个非负的精度误差ε≥0，在此容忍范围内构建更小的决策树，通过"蒸馏"控制器的本质来保证ε-最优性，从而实现可调节的简化解释。

Result: 该方法构建的决策树比现有最先进技术（dtControl2）小几个数量级，显著降低了复杂度，同时通过ε参数提供了可控的精度-可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: 通过允许可控的精度损失，可以大幅简化决策树控制器表示，使其更容易被人类理解，同时仍能保证近似的性能保证，为大规模复杂系统的可解释AI控制提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [317] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种求解器引导的大语言模型框架，用于多仓库库存分配中的运筹学（OR）配方选择问题。该框架将每个OR专家编码为不同的MIP配方，通过SFT进行模式学习，再使用MIP求解器评估历史实例，将分配质量差距转化为加权偏好数据，最后通过GRPO优化选择器。在京东数据集上，GRPO将Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%，分配准确率比基线提高12.57个百分点，与事后最优的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常被建模为混合整数规划（MIP）问题，但没有单一配方能够一致地匹配由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动性所引发的异构实例级场景。因此需要研究实例级的运筹学配方选择问题，为每个分配实例从候选OR专家库中分配一个可执行的配方。

Method: 提出求解器引导的大语言模型框架：1）每个OR专家对应一个编码不同分配优先级的MIP配方；2）首先构建平衡的专家条件SFT记录进行模式学习；3）使用MIP求解器评估历史实例，将求解器评估的分配质量差距转化为边际加权身份偏好优化（IPO）偏好和每个实例的专家分数元数据；4）在组相对策略优化（GRPO）中利用这些元数据进行奖励查找，为采样的响应分配奖励。

Result: 在京东多仓库库存分配实例上，GRPO相对于SFT+IPO选择器显著提高了专家选择准确率，并产生了更高的实际分配质量。Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。分配准确率比基线提高12.57个百分点，优于SFT+IPO选择器和最佳固定OR专家，与事后最优的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器引导的LLM框架结合GRPO能够有效解决多仓库库存分配中的OR配方选择问题，显著提升分配质量和选择准确率，缩小与事后最优的差距。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [318] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CHARM，一个多模态图基础模型，通过分层上下文建模实现零样本迁移。它用分层图上下文替换孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，并利用模态感知的图上下文编码器将多模态信息与图结构整合，转换为大语言模型的图令牌，在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的图常包含文本、图像等多模态信息，而现有的图基础模型大多需要下游微调，基于大语言模型的图方法主要处理单模态图或单一领域任务。零样本迁移在多模态图上尚未充分探索，面临两个挑战：模型需泛化单模态知识并捕获可迁移的跨模态关系；无目标域微调时，节点表示与领域特定结构和模态特定特征纠缠，难以在未见领域共享概念。

Method: 提出CHARM，一个多模态图基础模型，采用分层上下文建模。将孤立原始节点替换为分层图上下文，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系；设计模态感知的图上下文编码器，整合多模态信息与图结构，并将表示转换为大语言模型的图令牌。

Result: 在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致的改进。

Conclusion: CHARM通过分层上下文建模有效解决了零样本多模态图迁移中的领域差异和模态纠缠问题，减少了对目标域监督或适应的依赖，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [319] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过行为实验和演化模型，研究AI竞赛中安全与速度的权衡。实验发现，不安全开发行为更多由竞赛的动态策略状态（对手行为、领先/落后位置、首轮选择）驱动，而非个人风险偏好。演化模型表明，条件性不安全行为在竞赛动态中可能被偏好。政策应关注减少竞争压力、促进合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术竞赛（如AI开发）中，速度与安全存在张力：参与者可能为了更快发展而采取风险更大的不安全开发方式，即使这可能造成长期危害。现有的争论常认为竞争压力会激励更危险、更少安全意识的开发，但缺乏对个体行为机制的深入理解。本文旨在通过实验和模型探究不安全开发行为的真正驱动因素。

Method: 采用框架化行为实验，模拟理想化的AI竞赛：配对参与者反复在安全与不安全开发之间选择，时间 horizon 不确定。不安全开发提供更快进度和更高即时收益，但累积私人风险，风险上限分为10%、60%、90%三种处理。保持竞赛结构不变，仅改变最大风险。同时引入一个简化演化模型，包含四种策略（始终安全、始终不安全、条件性安全、条件性反社会安全），以解释实验数据。

Result: 预先注册的风险水平比较以及风险偏好作用未得到数据支持。探索性分析表明：不安全行为受竞赛战略状态影响更大——参与者更可能在对手选择不安全后也选择不安全；领先时减少不安全选择，落后时增加；首轮选择可预测后续行为。演化模型复现了处理效应，并表明条件性不安全行为在竞赛动态中可能被演化选择所青睐。

Conclusion: 不安全开发行为可能源于早期的行为动量、对手行为及对落后的恐惧，而非单纯的风险偏好。因此，AI开发政策应侧重于降低竞争压力和促进合作，而非仅关注个体风险管理。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [320] [AVE-Compass: Towards Holistic Evaluation for Audio-Video Editing Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24821)
*Yuqing Wen,Yukai Huang,Qianqian Xie,Jiangtao Wu,Yibin Lin,Yikai Gu,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了AVE-Compass基准和AVE-Agent框架，用于评估和提升音频-视觉联合视频编辑中的跨模态一致性。AVE-Compass包含145个源视频、196条音视频耦合编辑指令和2688个细粒度检查项，从指令遵循、保真度、真实感和编辑意图四个维度进行评估。AVE-Agent通过模块化分解、自我反思和评估反馈迭代优化编辑结果，在联合编辑中提升了指令执行、保真度和音视频对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑基准主要评估视觉变换（静音片段）或单独音频编辑，缺乏对复杂音视频联合编辑和跨模态一致性的评估。真实世界视频中音频和视觉信号紧密耦合，编辑一个模态通常需要另一个模态的协调变化。

Method: 构建AVE-Compass基准，使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）基于检查表评判指令遵循、保真度、真实感和编辑意图，并辅以自动跨模态、视频和音频指标。提出AVE-Agent模块化框架，将复杂指令分解为依赖子任务，通过自我反思和评估反馈迭代改进编辑结果。

Result: 评估表明现有最先进模型在执行跨模态指令和保持非目标内容方面仍有困难。AVE-Agent在联合编辑中提升了指令执行、保真度和音视频对齐，同时保持了有竞争力的感知质量。

Conclusion: 音视频联合编辑仍需进一步研究，AVE-Agent为复杂跨模态编辑提供了有效框架，但整体性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: While instruction-based video editing has advanced rapidly, real-world videos contain tightly coupled audio and visual signals, and editing one modality often requires coordinated changes in the other. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate visual transformations on silent clips or isolated audio editing, leaving complex audio-visual editing and cross-modal consistency underexplored. We introduce AVE-Compass, a comprehensive benchmark with 145 curated source videos, 196 audio-visually coupled editing instructions, and 2,688 fine-grained checklist items. It evaluates Instruction Following, Fidelity Preserving, Realism, and Editing Intent through checklist-based MLLM judging and a dedicated realism rubric, complemented by automated cross-modal, video, and audio metrics. Extensive evaluation shows that state-of-the-art models still struggle to execute cross-modal instructions while preserving non-target content. We further propose AVE-Agent, a modular agent framework that decomposes complex instructions into dependent subtasks and iteratively improves editing results through self-reflection and evaluator feedback. AVE-Agent improves instruction execution, Fidelity Preserving, and audio-visual alignment in joint editing while maintaining competitive perceptual quality.

</details>
